### (Part Four) ^7-4-0

#### Conditional Relations of the Pairs in the Positive Method by States ^7-4-1-0

##### The Defilement Cluster ^7-4-1-1-0

###### The Defilement Pair ^7-4-1-1-1-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-2-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-3-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-4-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-4-1-0

Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth [variant: thīnaṃ (Sī., Syā.)], restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, conceit, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, conceit, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on hatred, delusion, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on hatred, delusion, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on doubt, delusion, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on restlessness, delusion, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing. (1)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on defilements, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement arise because of the root condition – dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness (cycle). (3) ^7-34-1

Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate that is not a defilement, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element…. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates that are not defilements, defilements. (2)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate that is not a defilement, three aggregates, defilements and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates…. (3) ^7-34-2

Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on greed and the associated aggregates, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing (cycle). (1)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate that is not a defilement and on defilements, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on defilements and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate that is not a defilement and on greed, three aggregates, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates and… (cycle, abbreviated). (3) ^7-34-3

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-5-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-6-0

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine (nine in every case), result: one… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-34-4

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-7-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-8-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-8-1-0

Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – dependent on doubt, delusion; dependent on restlessness, delusion. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots that is not a defilement, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… (up to the non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and on doubt, delusion accompanied by doubt; dependent on the aggregates accompanied by restlessness and on restlessness, delusion accompanied by restlessness. (1) ^7-34-5

####### *The Not-Object Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-8-2-0

Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the object condition – dependent on defilements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the object condition – dependent on the aggregates that are not defilements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base (up to the non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the object condition – dependent on defilements and the associated aggregates, matter produced by consciousness; dependent on defilements and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1)
Not because of the predominance condition… not because of the proximity condition… not because of the contiguity condition… not because of the mutuality condition… not because of the strong-support condition. ^7-34-6

####### *The Not-Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-8-3-0

Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, conceit, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, conceit, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on greed, delusion, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on doubt, delusion, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing; dependent on restlessness, delusion, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing (in the immaterial realm there is no series based on hatred). (1)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on defilements, the associated aggregates; dependent on defilements, matter produced by consciousness (in this way nine questions are to be worked out), not because of the post-arising condition, not because of the repetition condition. ^7-34-7

####### *The Not-Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-8-4-0

Dependent on a state that is a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on defilements, the associated volition. (1)
Dependent on a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on the aggregates that are not defilements, the associated volition; the external… produced by nutriment… produced by temperature…. (1)
Dependent on a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the kamma condition – dependent on defilements and the associated aggregates, the associated volition. (1) (In this way all the conditions are to be worked out.) ^7-34-8

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-9-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-10-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-10-1-0

Not-root: four, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: three, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: nine, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-34-9

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-12-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-13-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-14-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-14-1-0

With a state that is a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition… three (same as the Dependence Section).
With a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is not a defilement arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is not a defilement as condition, three aggregates… (up to the internal great elements) with the physical base as condition, the aggregates that are not defilements. (1)
With a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates that are not defilements as condition, defilements; with the physical base as condition, defilements. (2)
With a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is not a defilement as condition, three aggregates, defilements and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition…; with the physical base as condition, defilements; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, defilements and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-34-10

With a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – with greed and the associated aggregates as condition, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing (cycle). With greed and the physical base as condition, defilements. (1)
With a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is not a defilement arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is not a defilement and defilements as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates and…; with defilements and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with defilements and the physical base as condition, the aggregates that are not defilements. (2)
With a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate that is not a defilement and greed as condition, three aggregates, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates and… (cycle). With greed and the physical base as condition, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing and the associated aggregates (cycle). (3)
(In the object condition, with what is not a defilement as the base, the five kinds of sense-consciousness are to be worked out.) ^7-34-11

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-15-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-16-0

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine (nine in every case), result: one… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-34-12

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-17-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-18-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-18-1-0

With a state that is a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – with doubt as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt; with restlessness as condition, delusion accompanied by restlessness. (1)
With a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is not a defilement arises not because of the root condition (up to the non-percipient beings) – with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… the body base…; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates without roots that are not defilements. (1)
With a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
With a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement as condition, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition – with doubt, the associated aggregates and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt; with restlessness, the associated aggregates and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by restlessness (abbreviated). (1) ^7-34-13

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-21-0

####### *1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-21-1-0

Conjoined with a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises because of the root condition – conjoined with greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing. (Cycle. In this way nine questions are to be worked out.)
Root: nine, object: nine (nine in every case), result: one… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method.
Conjoined with a state that is a defilement, a state that is a defilement arises not because of the root condition (in this way four not-root questions are to be worked out.)
Not-root: four, not-predominance: nine, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: three, not-result: nine, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: nine.
The negative method. ^7-34-15

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-22-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U419

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-23-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-24-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-25-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-1-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of root condition – defilements that are roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated defilements. (The base should be asked.) Defilements that are roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (The base should be asked.) Defilements that are roots are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, defilements and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of root condition – roots that are not defilements are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-34-16

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-2-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of object condition – with defilements as object, defilements arise. (The base should be asked.) With defilements as object, aggregates that are not defilements arise. (The base should be asked.) With defilements as object, defilements and the associated aggregates arise. (3) ^7-34-17

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of object condition – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… good deeds done earlier… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust… wrong view… doubt… restlessness… distress arises; noble ones, having emerged from the path… for fruit and for adverting, a condition by way of object condition; the eye… the physical base; one sees the aggregates that are not defilements with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element… for knowledge of the future and for adverting, a condition by way of object condition. (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of object condition – giving… having emerged from jhāna, one enjoys and delights in the jhāna; with that as object, lust… wrong view… doubt… restlessness arises; when jhāna has declined, distress arises in one who feels remorse; the eye… the physical base; one enjoys and delights in the aggregates that are not defilements; with that as object, lust… distress arises. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of object condition – giving… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… the physical base; one enjoys and delights in the aggregates that are not defilements; with that as object, defilements and the associated aggregates arise. (3)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of object condition… three. ^7-34-18

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-3-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving weight to defilements, defilements arise… three (object-predominance only). (3)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties and given them weight, one reviews them, enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; earlier… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path giving it weight… a condition, by way of predominance condition, for fruit; the eye… the physical base; giving weight to the aggregates that are not defilements, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… jhāna… the eye… the physical base; giving weight to the aggregates that are not defilements, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated defilements. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… jhāna… the eye… the physical base; giving weight to the aggregates that are not defilements, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, defilements and the associated aggregates arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates, for defilements and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of predominance condition… three (object-predominance only). (3) ^7-34-19

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-25-4-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of proximity condition – preceding defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not defilements; defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-34-20

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates that are not defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not defilements… is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates that are not defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements; adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for defilements. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates that are not defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements and the associated aggregates; adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for defilements and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-34-21

A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of proximity condition – preceding defilements and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding defilements and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates that are not defilements; defilements and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding defilements and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defilements and the associated aggregates. (3)
A condition by way of contiguity condition, a condition by way of co-arising condition, a condition by way of mutuality condition, a condition by way of support condition. ^7-34-22

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-5-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – defilements are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for defilements… three. ^7-34-23

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; virtue… wisdom… lust… hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing… bodily pleasure…; relying on lodging, one gives a gift… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; virtue… relying on lodging, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for defilements. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; virtue… relying on lodging, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for defilements and the associated aggregates. (3)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of strong-support condition… three. ^7-34-24

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-6-0

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base; one sees it with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates that are not defilements. (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base; one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust… distress arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for defilements. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base; one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, defilements and the associated aggregates arise. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for defilements and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-34-25

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-25-7-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated), a condition by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-34-26

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-8-0

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of kamma condition – volition that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for defilements. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of kamma condition – volition that is not a defilement is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates, for defilements and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-34-27

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-25-9-0

A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of result condition… one… by way of nutriment condition… three… by way of faculty condition… three… by way of jhāna condition… three… by way of path condition… nine… by way of association condition… nine. ^7-34-28

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-4-1-1-25-10-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of dissociation condition. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for defilements. (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of dissociation condition. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for defilements and the associated aggregates. (3)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated. To be expanded.). ^7-34-29

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-25-11-0

A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of presence condition… one (same as the Dependence Section). (1)
A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (2)
A state that is a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition (same as the Dependence Section). (3)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). (1)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated. Co-arising is the same as co-arising, pre-arising the same as pre-arising.) (2)
A state that is not a defilement is a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (co-arising is the same as co-arising, pre-arising the same as pre-arising). (3) ^7-34-30

Co-arising – greed and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing (cycle). A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – defilements and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising – defilements and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates that are not defilements. Post-arising – defilements and the associated aggregates are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Post-arising – defilements, the associated aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – defilements, the associated aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement are a condition for a state that is a defilement and a state that is not a defilement by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate that is not a defilement and greed are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates, for matter produced by consciousness and for delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing… two aggregates and…. Co-arising – greed and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing and the associated aggregates (cycle). (3)
A condition by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-34-31

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-53-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-54-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-54-1-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of root condition – roots defiled are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of root condition – roots defiled are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled and a state not defiled by way of root condition – roots defiled are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of root condition – roots not defiled are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-34-51

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-54-2-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of object condition – one enjoys and delights in lust; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises; doubt… restlessness… distress arises; one enjoys and delights in wrong view… (the same as the Wholesome Triad); with doubt as object… with restlessness as object… distress arises; wrong view… doubt… restlessness arises. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of object condition – noble ones review the abandoned defilements, the suppressed defilements… the defilements that occurred earlier… the defiled aggregates as impermanent… one sees them with insight; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses defiled consciousness; defiled aggregates are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and adverting. (2) ^7-34-52

A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of object condition – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… earlier… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna; noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path… a condition, by way of object condition, for adverting; the eye… the physical base; the aggregates not defiled as impermanent… one sees with insight; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… for knowledge of the future and for adverting, a condition by way of object condition. (1)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… the physical base; one enjoys and delights in the aggregates not defiled; with that as object, lust… distress arises. (2) ^7-34-53

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-54-3-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to lust, one enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to wrong view, one enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance defiled is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance defiled is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled and a state not defiled by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance defiled is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-34-54

A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… earlier… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, giving it weight; noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight… nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for fruit. Co-arising predominance – predominance not defiled is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… earlier… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… the physical base; giving weight to the aggregates not defiled, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. (2) ^7-34-55

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-54-4-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of proximity condition – preceding defiled aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following defiled aggregates. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of proximity condition – defiled aggregates are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2) ^7-34-56

A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not defiled are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not defiled… is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of proximity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for defiled aggregates. (2)
A condition by way of contiguity condition… four… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-34-57

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-54-5-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; hatred… relying on longing, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; lust… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for longing. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one gives a gift… produces attainment; hatred… relying on longing, one gives a gift… produces attainment; lust… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for wisdom, for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-34-58

A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… produces attainment; virtue… wisdom, bodily pleasure… bodily pain… temperature… food… relying on lodging, one gives a gift… produces attainment; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; virtue… wisdom… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… temperature… food… relying on lodging, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for longing. (2) ^7-34-59

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-54-8-0

Co-arising – defiled volition is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition not defiled is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. A state defiled and a state not defiled are a condition for a state defiled by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate defiled and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… (abbreviated). (1)
A state defiled and a state not defiled are a condition for a state not defiled by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Post-arising – defiled aggregates and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – defiled aggregates and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
A condition by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-34-66

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-55-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-56-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-56-1-0

Root: four, object: four, predominance: five, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: four, result: one, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: seven. ^7-34-67

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-56-2-0

A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. (1)
A state defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (2)
A state defiled is a condition for a state defiled and a state not defiled by way of co-arising condition. (3) ^7-34-68

A state not defiled is a condition for a state not defiled by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. (1)
A state not defiled is a condition for a state defiled by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state defiled and a state not defiled are a condition for a state defiled, co-arising… pre-arising. (1)
A state defiled and a state not defiled are a condition for a state not defiled, co-arising… post-arising… nutriment… faculty. (2) ^7-34-69

###### The Associated-with-the-Defilements Pair ^7-4-1-1-61-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-62-0

Dependent on a state associated with the defilements, a state associated with the defilements arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate associated with the defilements, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates…. (1)
Dependent on a state associated with the defilements, a state dissociated from the defilements arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates associated with the defilements, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
(The Associated-with-the-Defilements Pair is the same as the Defiled Pair, without difference.)
The Associated-with-the-Defilements Pair is finished. ^7-34-73

###### The Defilement and Subject-to-the-Defilement Pair ^7-4-1-1-63-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-64-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-64-1-0

Dependent on a state both a defilement and subject to defilement, a state both a defilement and subject to defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing (cycle). (1)
Dependent on a state both a defilement and subject to defilement, a state subject to defilement but not a defilement arises because of the root condition – dependent on defilements, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state both a defilement and subject to defilement, states both a defilement and subject to defilement, and subject to defilement but not a defilement, arise because of the root condition – dependent on greed, delusion, wrong view, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-34-74

###### 2-6. The Co-arising, Condition, Support, Conjoined and Association Sections ^7-4-1-1-65-0

(In the same way, the Dependence Section, the Co-arising Section, the Condition Section, the Support Section, the Conjoined Section and the Association Section are the same as the Defilement Pair. Without difference. The reference differs.) ^7-U423

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-66-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-67-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-68-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-68-1-0

A state both a defilement and subject to defilement is a condition for a state both a defilement and subject to defilement by way of root condition – roots that are both defilements and subject to defilement are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated defilements (in this way four; the same as the Defilement Pair). (4) ^7-34-75

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-68-2-0

A state both a defilement and subject to defilement is a condition for a state both a defilement and subject to defilement by way of object condition – with defilements as object, defilements arise. (The base should be worked out.) With defilements as object, aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements arise. (The base should be worked out.) With defilements as object, defilements and the associated aggregates arise. (3) ^7-34-76

A state subject to defilement but not a defilement is a condition for a state subject to defilement but not a defilement by way of object condition – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… good deeds done earlier… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… restlessness arises; when jhāna has declined, distress arises in one who feels remorse; noble ones review change-of-lineage, review cleansing; the eye… the physical base; one sees the aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements with insight as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… is a condition, by way of object condition, for adverting (the other two are the same as the Defilement Pair; the object combinations too are the same as the Defilement Pair). ^7-34-77

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-68-3-0

A state both a defilement and subject to defilement is a condition for a state both a defilement and subject to defilement by way of predominance condition – object-predominance… three.
A state subject to defilement but not a defilement is a condition for a state subject to defilement but not a defilement by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… good deeds done earlier… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, giving it weight; trainees review change-of-lineage giving it weight, review cleansing giving it weight; the eye… the physical base; giving weight to the aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements, aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance that is subject to defilement but not a defilement is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (The other two are also the same as the Defilement Pair. So too the predominance combinations.) ^7-34-78

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-68-4-0

A state both a defilement and subject to defilement is a condition for a state both a defilement and subject to defilement by way of proximity condition… three (the same as the Defilement Pair).
A state subject to defilement but not a defilement is a condition for a state subject to defilement but not a defilement by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for aggregates subject to defilement but not defilements.
(The other two proximity conditions are the same as the Defilement Pair, without difference. The proximity combinations too, and all the conditions, are the same as the Defilement Pair, without difference. In strong-support there is nothing supramundane; this pair is the same as the Defilement Pair, without difference.)
The Defilement and Subject-to-the-Defilement Pair is finished. ^7-34-79

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-80-4-0

Not-root: four, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-10

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-109-0

####### *1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-109-1-0

Conjoined with a state to be abandoned by seeing, a state to be abandoned by seeing arises because of the root condition (abbreviated). (1)
Conjoined with a state not to be abandoned by seeing, a state not to be abandoned by seeing arises because of the root condition (abbreviated). (1)
Root: two, object: two, predominance: two, non-disappearance: two.
The positive method. ^7-35-11

Conjoined with a state to be abandoned by seeing, a state to be abandoned by seeing arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by doubt. (1)
Conjoined with a state not to be abandoned by seeing, a state not to be abandoned by seeing arises not because of the root condition (abbreviated). (1)
Not-root: two, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: two.
The negative method. ^7-35-12

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-111-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-112-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-113-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-1-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of root condition… three.
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of root condition – roots not to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-13

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-2-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – one enjoys and delights in lust to be abandoned by seeing; with that as object, lust to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view… doubt arises, distress to be abandoned by seeing arises; one enjoys and delights in wrong view to be abandoned by seeing; with that as object, lust to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view… doubt… distress arises; with doubt as object, doubt arises, wrong view… distress arises; with distress to be abandoned by seeing as object, distress to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises. (1)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – noble ones review the abandoned defilements to be abandoned by seeing, the defilements habitually indulged in before… one sees the aggregates to be abandoned by seeing with insight as impermanent…; with knowledge of others' minds one knows the mind of one who possesses a mind to be abandoned by seeing; the aggregates to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and adverting. (2) ^7-35-14

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – after giving a gift… undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust not to be abandoned by seeing arises; restlessness… distress not to be abandoned by seeing arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path… it is a condition, by way of object condition, for fruit and for adverting; noble ones review the abandoned defilements not to be abandoned by seeing, the suppressed defilements… those of before… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing: one sees them with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust not to be abandoned by seeing arises, restlessness… distress not to be abandoned by seeing arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… it is a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – after giving a gift… undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties… good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, the aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing; with that as object, lust to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view… doubt… distress to be abandoned by seeing arises. (2) ^7-35-15

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-3-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to lust to be abandoned by seeing, one enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to wrong view…. Co-arising predominance – predominance to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing and a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-16

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – after giving a gift… undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties… good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, giving weight to the jhāna, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust not to be abandoned by seeing arises; noble ones, having emerged from the path, giving weight to the path… it is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for fruit; the eye… giving weight to the physical base, the aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust not to be abandoned by seeing arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – after giving a gift… the jhāna… the eye… giving weight to the physical base, the aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view arises. (2) ^7-35-17

####### *The Proximity Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-4-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates to be abandoned by seeing. (1)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of proximity condition – aggregates to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing… are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for attainment of fruition. (The base should be worked out.) Adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for aggregates to be abandoned by seeing. (2)
It is a condition by way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-18

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-5-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust to be abandoned by seeing, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; relying on hatred… on delusion… on wrong view… on longing, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; lust to be abandoned by seeing… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust to be abandoned by seeing… for longing. (2)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust to be abandoned by seeing, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; relying on hatred… on delusion… on wrong view… on longing, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; lust to be abandoned by seeing… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… wisdom, for lust not to be abandoned by seeing… hatred… delusion… conceit… longing… bodily pleasure… attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-19

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, fosters conceit; relying on virtue… on wisdom, on lust not to be abandoned by seeing… on hatred… on delusion… on conceit… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, fosters conceit; faith… wisdom, lust not to be abandoned by seeing… longing… bodily pleasure… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… wisdom, for lust not to be abandoned by seeing… longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path, for attainment of fruition. (2)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue… on wisdom, one adopts a wrong view; relying on lust not to be abandoned by seeing… on hatred… on delusion… on conceit… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on lodging, one kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust to be abandoned by seeing… for hatred… for delusion… for wrong view… for longing. (2) ^7-35-20

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-6-0

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one sees the physical base with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust not to be abandoned by seeing… restlessness… distress not to be abandoned by seeing arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. The visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates not to be abandoned by seeing. (1)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base; with that as object, lust to be abandoned by seeing… wrong view… doubt… distress to be abandoned by seeing arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates to be abandoned by seeing. (2) ^7-35-21

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-113-7-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated). (1)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of post-arising condition (abbreviated)… by way of repetition condition… two. ^7-35-22

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-8-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of kamma condition – volition to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. From a different moment – volition to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates and matter produced by kamma. (2)
A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing and a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of kamma condition – volition to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-23

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates and matter produced by kamma. ^7-35-24

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-113-9-0

A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of result condition… one… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two. ^7-35-25

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-4-1-1-113-10-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated).
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of dissociation condition. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for aggregates to be abandoned by seeing. (2) ^7-35-26

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-113-11-0

A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition (the same as the dependence section). A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing and a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition (the same as the dependence section). (3)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition. Pre-arising – the eye… (abbreviated, the same as pre-arising). (2) ^7-35-27

A state to be abandoned by seeing and a state not to be abandoned by seeing are a condition for a state to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate to be abandoned by seeing and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and…. (1)
A state to be abandoned by seeing and a state not to be abandoned by seeing are a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – aggregates to be abandoned by seeing and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates to be abandoned by seeing and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – aggregates to be abandoned by seeing and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
It is a condition by way of absence condition, by way of disappearance condition, by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-35-28

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: four, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four (four in every case), not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-33

###### The Pair to Be Abandoned by Development ^7-4-1-1-120-0

###### 1-6. The Dependence Section and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-121-0

####### *1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-121-1-0

Dependent on a state to be abandoned by development, a state to be abandoned by development arises because of the root condition (it should be expanded in the same way as the Pair to Be Abandoned by Seeing, with no difference).
Root: five… non-disappearance: five.
The positive method.
Dependent on a state to be abandoned by development, a state to be abandoned by development arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by restlessness.
Dependent on a state not to be abandoned by development, a state not to be abandoned by development arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots not to be abandoned by development… (up to the non-percipient beings) dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by doubt (abbreviated).
Not-root: two… not-disappearance: three.
The negative method.
(In the negative method of the condition section, under the not-root condition, three for those accompanied by restlessness; delusion should be extracted. All the sections are the same as the Pair to Be Abandoned by Seeing; the negative method regarding restlessness is also different.) ^7-35-35

A state to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. A state to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state to be abandoned by development by way of proximity condition… four (the same as the Pair to Be Abandoned by Seeing; development has no difference)… by way of contiguity condition… four… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-41

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-124-5-0

(1)
A state not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state to be abandoned by development by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit… relying on virtue… on wisdom… on lust… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on lodging, one fosters conceit; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust to be abandoned by development… hatred… delusion… conceit… longing. (2) ^7-35-43

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-124-6-0

A state not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by development by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one sees the physical base with insight as impermanent… enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust not to be abandoned by development arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises; distress not to be abandoned by development arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates not to be abandoned by development. (1)
A state not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state to be abandoned by development by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base; with that as object, lust to be abandoned by development arises, restlessness arises, distress to be abandoned by development arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates to be abandoned by development. (2) ^7-35-44

It is a condition by way of post-arising condition… two, by way of repetition condition… two, by way of kamma condition – volition to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. (The base should be worked out.) Volition to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base should be worked out.) Volition to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by development by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition not to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition not to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates and matter produced by kamma… by way of result condition… one… by way of non-disappearance condition. (All the conditions are the same as in the Pair to Be Abandoned by Seeing; development has no difference.) ^7-35-45

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-125-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-126-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-126-1-0

Root: four, object: four, predominance: five, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: four, result: one, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: seven.
(The negative exposition should be divided in the same way as in the Pair to Be Abandoned by Seeing. The three counts should also be counted in this way.)
The Pair to Be Abandoned by Development is finished. ^7-35-46

a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of root condition – roots with roots to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates. a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of root condition – roots with roots not to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; delusion accompanied by doubt is a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. ####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-150-2-0

a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – with the aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing as object, aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing as object, aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by seeing and delusion arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing as object, aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion arise. (3) ^7-35-70

a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – after giving a gift… undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust with roots not to be abandoned by seeing arises, restlessness arises, distress with roots not to be abandoned by seeing arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path… noble ones review the abandoned defilements with roots not to be abandoned by seeing, they review suppressed defilements… they know the defilements they habitually indulged in before; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by seeing and delusion, as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust with roots not to be abandoned by seeing arises, restlessness arises, distress with roots not to be abandoned by seeing arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… it is a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of the future, for adverting and for delusion.
a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – a gift… jhāna… the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by seeing and delusion; with that as object, lust with roots to be abandoned by seeing arises, wrong view… doubt… distress with roots to be abandoned by seeing arises.
a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing and a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – the eye… with the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by seeing and delusion as object, aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion arise. a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing and a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of object condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion as object, aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing arise.
