With the aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion as object, aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion arise. (3) ^7-35-72

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-150-3-0

a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing, aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing and a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-73

a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of predominance condition. (2) ^7-35-74

Preceding aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following delusion accompanied by doubt; aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base to be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by doubt and for delusion. (3)
A condition by way of contiguity condition… nine… by way of co-arising condition… nine… by way of mutuality condition… six… by way of support condition… nine. ^7-35-77

a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. (In the remaining two, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support.) (The base to be worked out.) (The base to be worked out.) Aggregates with roots to be abandoned by seeing are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates accompanied by doubt and for delusion. (3) ^7-35-78

a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. (1)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. (2)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by seeing and a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Aggregates accompanied by doubt and delusion are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by seeing and for delusion. a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by seeing by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. (Abbreviated. The pre-arising physical base is abbreviated.) (1)

From a different moment – volition with roots to be abandoned by seeing is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. ####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-160-1-0

A state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of root condition… six… (the same as the Roots-Abandoned-by-Seeing Pair). ^7-35-94

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-160-2-0

A state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of object condition… three (the same as the Roots-Abandoned-by-Seeing Pair as far as the phrase "with it as object" is concerned). ^7-35-95

a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of object condition – after giving a gift… undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust with roots not to be abandoned by development arises, wrong view… doubt… distress with roots not to be abandoned by development arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path… it is a condition, by way of object condition, for fruit and for adverting; noble ones review the abandoned defilements with roots not to be abandoned by development… the defilements habitually indulged in before… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by development and delusion, as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust with roots not to be abandoned by development arises, wrong view… doubt… distress with roots not to be abandoned by development arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… it is a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of the future, for adverting and for delusion. (1)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of object condition – a gift… virtue… jhāna… the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by development and delusion; with that as object, lust with roots to be abandoned by development arises, restlessness… distress with roots to be abandoned by development arises. (2)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development and a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of object condition – the eye… with the physical base, the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by development and delusion as object, aggregates accompanied by restlessness and delusion arise (all three object combinations are to be worked out). (3) ^7-35-96

####### *The Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-160-3-0

a state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to lust with roots to be abandoned by development, one enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust with roots to be abandoned by development arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
a state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to lust with roots to be abandoned by development, one enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust with roots not to be abandoned by development arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development and a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-97

a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Co-arising predominance – predominance with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of predominance condition – object-predominance – a gift… jhāna… the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, giving weight to the aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by development; giving it weight, lust with roots to be abandoned by development arises. (2)
(In the proximity condition, on account of "with roots not to be abandoned by development", delusion accompanied by doubt is not to be worked out; delusion accompanied by restlessness is to be worked out.) A condition by way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… nine… by way of mutuality condition… six… by way of support condition… nine. ^7-35-98

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-160-4-0

a state with roots to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – aggregates with roots to be abandoned by development are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with roots to be abandoned by development. (The base to be worked out.) Aggregates with roots to be abandoned by development are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with roots not to be abandoned by development and for delusion; desire and lust for one's own goods are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for desire and lust for another's goods; desire and lust for one's own possessions are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for desire and lust for another's possessions. (The base to be worked out.) Aggregates with roots to be abandoned by development are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates accompanied by restlessness and for delusion. (3) ^7-35-99

a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue… on wisdom… on lust with roots not to be abandoned by development… on hatred… on delusion… on wrong view… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on lodging, one gives a gift… kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for wisdom… for lust with roots not to be abandoned by development… for hatred… for delusion… for wrong view… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit… faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust with roots to be abandoned by development… for hatred… for delusion… for conceit… for longing. a state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots to be abandoned by development and a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. ####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-160-5-0

A state with roots not to be abandoned by development is a condition for a state with roots not to be abandoned by development by way of pre-arising condition… three… by way of post-arising condition… three… by way of repetition condition… nine… by way of kamma condition (in the analysis of what is not to be abandoned by development, kamma from a different moment is obtained)… by way of not-disappearance condition. (Abbreviated. As in the Roots-Abandoned-by-Seeing Pair, so in the Roots-Abandoned-by-Development Pair the conditions, the negative method, the division and the counting are without difference.)
A state not to be abandoned by seeing is a condition for a state not to be abandoned by seeing…. (Finally, desire and lust for one's own goods should also be worked out.)

(2)
Dependent on a state without applied thought, a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought arise because of the root condition – dependent on applied thought, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; dependent on applied thought, aggregates with applied thought; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on applied thought, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on applied thought, aggregates with applied thought; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, aggregates with applied thought; dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, applied thought and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-35-103

(1)
Dependent on a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought, a state without applied thought arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates with applied thought and the physical base, applied thought. (2)

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-167-1-0

Dependent on a state with applied thought, a state with applied thought arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, with applied thought, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (The remaining two questions based on a state with applied thought are to be worked out; as to "without roots", there is no difference.) (3)
Dependent on a state without applied thought, a state without applied thought arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots, without applied thought, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… dependent on applied thought without roots, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on applied thought, matter produced by kamma; dependent on applied thought, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, applied thought; dependent on one great element…. (1)

(2)

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-168-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-168-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-107

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-169-0

With root as condition – not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three… not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-108

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-170-0

With not-root as condition – object: nine… proximity: nine… pre-arising: six, repetition: five, kamma: nine… path: three, association: nine (nine in every case). ^7-35-109

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-4-1-1-171-0

(The Co-arising Section is the same as the Dependence Section.) ^7-U434

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-172-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-173-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-174-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-174-1-0

With a state with applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the dependence section).
With a state without applied thought as condition, a state without applied thought arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate without applied thought as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition… with applied thought as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, with one aggregate without applied thought as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… with two aggregates as condition… at the moment of rebirth, with applied thought as condition, matter produced by kamma; with the aggregates as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, the aggregates; with applied thought as condition, the physical base; with the physical base as condition, applied thought; with one great element as condition, three great elements… with the physical base as condition, aggregates without applied thought; with the physical base as condition, applied thought.
With a state without applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition – with applied thought as condition, the associated aggregates; with the physical base as condition, aggregates with applied thought (two at the moment of rebirth too).
With a state without applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought arise because of the root condition – with applied thought as condition, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; with applied thought as condition, the associated aggregates; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates with applied thought; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the physical base as condition, applied thought and the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth… (at the moment of rebirth too it is just the same as in the course of existence). (3) ^7-35-110

With a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate with applied thought and applied thought as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition… with one aggregate with applied thought and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition… (two are to be worked out at the moment of rebirth). (1)
With a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought as condition, a state without applied thought arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with the aggregates with applied thought and the physical base as condition, applied thought; at the moment of rebirth… (three, at the moment of rebirth too). (2)
With a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate with applied thought and applied thought as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition… with one aggregate with applied thought, applied thought and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition… with the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; with one aggregate with applied thought and the physical base as condition, three aggregates and applied thought… with two aggregates as condition… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-35-111

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-175-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-175-1-0

Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine (nine in every case), non-disappearance: nine. ^7-35-112

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-176-0

####### *The Exposition* ^7-4-1-1-176-1-0

With a state with applied thought as condition, a state with applied thought arises not because of the root condition. (Nine questions are to be worked out. "Without roots" should be specified for only three. Delusion should be extracted; as in the dependence section, the questions are just the same as for the root condition, with the five kinds of sense-consciousness, in addition, delusion and applied thought.) ^7-35-113

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-177-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-177-1-0

Not-root: nine, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-114

###### The Support Section ^7-4-1-1-178-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out.) ^7-U435

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-179-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-180-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-180-1-0

Conjoined with a state with applied thought, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate with applied thought, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Conjoined with a state with applied thought, a state without applied thought arises because of the root condition – conjoined with the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought; at the moment of rebirth…. Conjoined with a state with applied thought, a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought arise because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate with applied thought, three aggregates and applied thought… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
Conjoined with a state without applied thought, a state without applied thought arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate without applied thought, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Conjoined with a state without applied thought, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition – the associated aggregates conjoined with applied thought; at the moment of rebirth…. (2)
Conjoined with a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought, a state with applied thought arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate with applied thought and applied thought, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1) (Abbreviated.)
Root: six, object: six, predominance: six (six in every case), non-disappearance: six.
The positive method.
Conjoined with a state with applied thought, a state with applied thought arises not because of the root condition. (In this way six questions are to be worked out, the same as the positive method; "without roots" should be specified for only three; delusion should be extracted.)
Not-root: six, not-predominance: six, not-pre-arising: six, not-post-arising: six, not-repetition: six, not-kamma: four, not-result: six, not-jhāna: one, not-path: six, not-dissociation: six.
The negative method. ^7-35-115

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-181-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U436

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-182-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-183-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-184-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-184-1-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of root condition – roots with applied thought are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. A state with applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of root condition – roots with applied thought are a condition, by way of root condition, for applied thought and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base to be worked out.) Roots with applied thought are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, applied thought and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (3)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of root condition – roots without applied thought are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-116

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-184-2-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of object condition – with the aggregates with applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with applied thought as object, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. (3) ^7-35-117

A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought, review the jhāna without applied thought; having emerged from the path, they review the path; having emerged from fruit, they review fruit; they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for the path without applied thought, for fruit and for applied thought; the eye… the physical base, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought, as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, applied thought arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element, knows by knowledge of others' minds the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness without applied thought; the base of infinite space… the base of nothingness… the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base…; aggregates without applied thought are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and applied thought; with the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought arise. (1)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, review fruit; they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path with applied thought, fruit and adverting; the eye… the physical base, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought, as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; with the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought arise. (2)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, review fruit; they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path with applied thought, fruit, adverting and applied thought; the eye… the physical base, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought, as impermanent… one sees with insight, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise; with the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. (3) ^7-35-118

A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought by way of object condition – with the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates without applied thought and applied thought arise. (The base to be worked out.) With the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought as object, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. (3) ^7-35-119

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-184-3-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought, aggregates with applied thought arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with applied thought is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state with applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought, applied thought arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance with applied thought is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for applied thought and for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with applied thought is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates, applied thought and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-120

A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, review fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the path without applied thought, for fruit and for applied thought; the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, giving weight to the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought; giving it weight, applied thought arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance without applied thought is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, review fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path with applied thought and fruit; the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, giving weight to the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought, aggregates with applied thought arise. (2)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from jhāna without applied thought… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, review fruit, giving it weight; they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path with applied thought, fruit and applied thought; the eye… one enjoys and delights in the physical base, giving weight to the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to the aggregates without applied thought and applied thought, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. (3)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought, aggregates with applied thought arise. (The base to be worked out.) Giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought, applied thought arises. (The base to be worked out.) Giving weight to the aggregates with applied thought and applied thought, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. (3) ^7-35-121

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-184-4-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought. (1)
A state with applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following applied thought; death-consciousness with applied thought is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for rebirth-consciousness without applied thought; adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the five kinds of sense-consciousness; aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence without applied thought; the preliminary work for the second jhāna is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the second jhāna; the preliminary work for the third jhāna… the preliminary work for the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the preliminary work for the divine eye… the preliminary work for the divine ear element… the preliminary work for knowledge of supernormal powers… the preliminary work for knowledge of others' minds… the preliminary work for knowledge of recollecting past lives… the preliminary work for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma is a condition for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma…; the preliminary work for knowledge of the future is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for knowledge of the future. Change-of-lineage is a condition for the path without applied thought… cleansing is a condition for the path without applied thought… conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition without applied thought. (2)
A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-122

A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding applied thought is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following applied thought; preceding aggregates without applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates without applied thought; the path without applied thought for fruit without applied thought… fruit without applied thought for fruit without applied thought… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for an attainment of fruition without applied thought. (1)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding applied thought is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought; death-consciousness without applied thought for rebirth-consciousness with applied thought, life-continuum without applied thought for adverting, aggregates without applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence with applied thought. (2)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding applied thought is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-123

A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought. (1)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following applied thought; death-consciousness with applied thought and applied thought for rebirth-consciousness without applied thought… adverting and applied thought for the five kinds of sense-consciousness… aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence without applied thought; the preliminary work for the second jhāna and applied thought… (the text written earlier should be seen in this way); conformity and applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for an attainment of fruition without applied thought. (2)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… nine… by way of mutuality condition… nine… by way of support condition… nine. ^7-35-124

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-184-5-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with applied thought. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates without applied thought and for applied thought. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-125

A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith without applied thought, one produces jhāna without applied thought, produces the path, produces higher knowledge, produces an attainment; relying on virtue without applied thought… on wisdom… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on temperature… on food… on lodging, and on applied thought, one produces jhāna without applied thought, produces the path… produces higher knowledge… produces an attainment; faith without applied thought… lodging and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith without applied thought… for wisdom… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path without applied thought… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of strong-support condition (all three kinds of strong support are to be worked out everywhere). Relying on faith without applied thought, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties; produces jhāna with applied thought, insight… the path… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue without applied thought… on lodging, and on applied thought, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith without applied thought… lodging and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith with applied thought… for wisdom… for lust… for longing, for the path with applied thought, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of strong-support condition – relying on faith without applied thought, one gives a gift… (all the terms written in the second pass are to be worked out) produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue… on wisdom… on lodging, and on applied thought, one gives a gift… kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith without applied thought… lodging and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith with applied thought… for wisdom… for lust… for longing, for the path with applied thought, for the attainment of fruition and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-126

A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought by way of strong-support condition – aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with applied thought. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates without applied thought and for applied thought. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-127

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-184-6-0

A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, applied thought arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates without applied thought and for applied thought.
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, aggregates with applied thought arise. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates with applied thought.
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, aggregates with applied thought and applied thought arise. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates with applied thought and for applied thought. (3) ^7-35-128

####### *The Post-arising and Repetition Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-184-7-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of post-arising condition (three, post-arising)… by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-35-129

####### *The Kamma Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-184-8-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition with applied thought is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition with applied thought is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates with applied thought. (Four in this way; both the co-arising and the from-a-different-moment are to be worked out.)
By way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… nine… by way of path condition… nine… by way of association condition… six. ^7-35-130

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated). A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for applied thought and the associated aggregates. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for applied thought and the associated aggregates. (3)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state without applied thought by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). ^7-35-131

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-184-9-0

A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of presence condition (one, the same as the dependence section). A state with applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state with applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of presence condition (the same as the dependence section). (3)
A state without applied thought is a condition for a state without applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated). A state without applied thought is a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – applied thought is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, applied thought is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth, the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for applied thought and the associated aggregates. Pre-arising – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, applied thought and the associated aggregates arise; the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for applied thought and the associated aggregates. (3) ^7-35-132

A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and…. Co-arising – one aggregate with applied thought and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… (at the moment of rebirth, the co-arising ones and also the two are to be worked out). (1)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state without applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Co-arising – aggregates with applied thought and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for applied thought (at the moment of rebirth, three). Post-arising – aggregates with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Post-arising – aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – aggregates with applied thought, applied thought and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (3)
A state with applied thought and a state without applied thought are a condition for a state with applied thought and a state without applied thought by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate with applied thought and applied thought are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates and…. Co-arising – one aggregate with applied thought and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates and for applied thought… two aggregates and… (at the moment of rebirth, also two). (3)
By way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-35-133

####### *1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-202-1-0

With a state with rapture as condition, a state with rapture arises because of the root condition. (Abbreviated. As in the positive Condition Section in the pair with applied thought, so in the course of life and at rebirth the nine questions are complete; rapture is without difference.)
Root: nine, object: nine, predominance: nine… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method.
With a state with rapture as condition, a state with rapture arises not because of the root condition… three (the same as the dependence section).
With a state without rapture as condition, a state without rapture arises not because of the root condition. (In the course of life and at rebirth it should be worked out like the Dependence Section, as far as non-percipient beings.) With the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates without roots and without rapture, and rapture; with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (Nine questions, the same as the positive method; only in the course of life, there is no rebirth, only one delusion.)
Not-root: nine, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: nine, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-147

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-209-1-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of root condition – roots with rapture are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. A state with rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of root condition – roots with rapture are a condition, by way of root condition, for rapture and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots with rapture are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates, for rapture and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of root condition – roots without rapture are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-149

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-209-2-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition – with the aggregates with rapture as object, aggregates with rapture arise. (The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates with rapture as object, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise. (The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates with rapture as object, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. (3) ^7-35-150

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of object condition – after giving a gift with consciousness without rapture, the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it with consciousness without rapture, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust without rapture arises, wrong view arises, doubt arises, restlessness arises, distress arises; having emerged from jhāna without rapture… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, one reviews the fruit with consciousness without rapture; noble ones review nibbāna with consciousness without rapture; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage without rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit, for adverting and for rapture; noble ones, with consciousness without rapture, the abandoned defilements without rapture… the suppressed defilements… earlier… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness without rapture, see with insight as impermanent… they enjoy and delight in them; with that as object, lust without rapture arises… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… the tangible base is a condition, by way of object condition, for body-consciousness; aggregates without rapture are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future, for adverting and for rapture; with the aggregates without rapture and rapture as object, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise. (1)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition – after giving a gift with consciousness without rapture, the precepts… the observance-day duties…, one reviews it with consciousness with rapture, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust with rapture arises, wrong view arises; having emerged from jhāna without rapture… having emerged from the path… having emerged from fruit, one reviews the fruit with consciousness with rapture; noble ones review nibbāna with consciousness with rapture; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage with rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit; noble ones, with consciousness with rapture, the abandoned defilements without rapture… the suppressed defilements… earlier… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness with rapture, see with insight as impermanent… they enjoy and delight in them; with that as object, lust with rapture arises, wrong view arises; with the aggregates without rapture and rapture as object, aggregates with rapture arise. (2)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of object condition – after giving a gift with consciousness without rapture, the precepts… the observance-day duties…, one reviews it with consciousness with rapture, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise; having emerged from jhāna without rapture… the path… the fruit, one reviews the fruit with consciousness with rapture; noble ones review nibbāna with consciousness with rapture; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage with rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit and for rapture; noble ones, with consciousness with rapture, review the abandoned defilements without rapture, the suppressed defilements… earlier… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness with rapture, see with insight as impermanent… they enjoy and delight in them; with that as object, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise; with the aggregates without rapture and rapture as object, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. (3) ^7-35-151

A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition – with the aggregates with rapture and rapture as object, aggregates with rapture arise. (The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates with rapture and rapture as object, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise.
(The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates with rapture and rapture as object, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. (3) ^7-35-152

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-209-3-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with rapture, aggregates with rapture arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with rapture is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. (1)
A state with rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with rapture, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with rapture is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for rapture and for matter produced by consciousness. (2)
A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with rapture, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance with rapture is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates, for rapture and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-153

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – with consciousness without rapture, after giving a gift… the precepts… the observance-day duties…, giving it weight, one reviews it with consciousness without rapture, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust without rapture arises, wrong view arises; having emerged from jhāna without rapture… the path… the fruit, with consciousness without rapture, giving weight to the fruit, one reviews it; noble ones, with consciousness without rapture, giving weight to nibbāna, review it; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage without rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness without rapture, giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving it weight, lust without rapture arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to the aggregates without rapture and rapture, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise. Co-arising predominance – predominance without rapture is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – with consciousness without rapture, after giving a gift… the precepts… the observance-day duties… (abbreviated) nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage with rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness with rapture, giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving it weight, lust with rapture arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to the aggregates without rapture and rapture, aggregates with rapture arise. (2)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving a gift… (abbreviated) nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage with rapture, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit and for rapture; the eye… the physical base, the aggregates without rapture and rapture, with consciousness with rapture, giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving weight to the aggregates without rapture and rapture, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. (3) ^7-35-154

A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – giving weight to the aggregates with rapture and rapture, aggregates with rapture arise. (The base should be worked out.) Giving weight to the aggregates with rapture and rapture, aggregates without rapture and rapture arise. (The base should be worked out.) Giving weight to the aggregates with rapture and rapture, aggregates with rapture and rapture arise. (3) ^7-35-155

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-209-4-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following rapture – death-consciousness with rapture for rebirth-consciousness without rapture, life-continuum with rapture for adverting, aggregates with rapture for emergence without rapture, the resultant mind-consciousness element accompanied by rapture for the functional mind-consciousness element, life-continuum with rapture for life-continuum without rapture, the wholesome and unwholesome with rapture for emergence without rapture, the functional for emergence, fruit is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3) ^7-35-156

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding rapture is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following rapture; preceding aggregates without rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates without rapture; conformity for change-of-lineage… for an attainment of fruition without rapture and for rapture is a condition, by way of proximity condition. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding rapture is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture; death-consciousness without rapture for rebirth-consciousness with rapture, adverting for aggregates with rapture, aggregates without rapture for emergence with rapture, the resultant mind element for the resultant mind-consciousness element with rapture, life-continuum without rapture for life-continuum with rapture, the wholesome and unwholesome without rapture for emergence with rapture, the functional for emergence, fruit is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence; for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for an attainment of fruition with rapture.
