A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding rapture is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3) ^7-35-157

A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture. (1)
A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state without rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following rapture; death-consciousness with rapture and rapture for rebirth-consciousness without rapture… life-continuum with rapture and rapture for adverting… aggregates with rapture and rapture for emergence without rapture… the resultant mind-consciousness element with rapture and rapture for the functional mind-consciousness element… life-continuum with rapture and rapture for life-continuum without rapture… the wholesome and unwholesome with rapture and rapture for emergence without rapture… the functional and rapture for emergence… fruit and rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2)
A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3)
By way of contiguity condition… nine… by way of co-arising condition… nine… by way of mutuality condition… nine… by way of support condition… nine. ^7-35-158

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-209-5-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with rapture. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates without rapture and for rapture. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3) ^7-35-159

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith without rapture, with consciousness without rapture one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties; produces jhāna without rapture… insight… the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue without rapture… on wisdom… on lust… on hatred… on delusion… on conceit… on wrong view… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on temperature… on food… on lodging… and on rapture, with consciousness without rapture one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith without rapture… lodging and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith without rapture… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path, for the attainment of fruition and for rapture. (1)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of strong-support condition (three kinds of strong support). Relying on faith without rapture, with consciousness with rapture one gives a gift… produces jhāna without rapture… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue without rapture… on lodging and on rapture, with consciousness with rapture one gives a gift… produces an attainment; with consciousness with rapture one takes what is not given, tells lies… speaks divisively… chatters idly… breaks into houses… carries off plunder… robs a single house… lies in ambush on the road… goes to another's wife… raids a village… raids a town; faith without rapture… lodging and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith with rapture… for wisdom, for lust, for delusion… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing… for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of strong-support condition (three kinds of strong support). Relying on faith without rapture, with consciousness with rapture one gives a gift… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue without rapture… on lodging and on rapture, with consciousness with rapture one gives a gift… produces an attainment; with consciousness with rapture one takes what is not given… (the same as the second pass) raids a town; faith without rapture… lodging and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith with rapture… for wisdom… for lust… for delusion… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing… for the path, for the attainment of fruition and for rapture. (3)
A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture by way of strong-support condition (all three kinds of strong support). Aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with rapture. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates without rapture and for rapture. (The base should be worked out.) Aggregates with rapture and rapture are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3) ^7-35-160

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-209-6-0

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness without rapture, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust without rapture… distress arises, rapture arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates without rapture and for rapture. (1)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness with rapture, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust with rapture arises, wrong view arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates with rapture. (2)
A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness with rapture, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, rapture and the associated aggregates arise. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates with rapture and for rapture. (3) ^7-35-161

####### *The Post-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-209-7-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of post-arising condition… three… by way of repetition condition… nine… by way of kamma condition… six (the co-arising and the from-a-different-moment are both to be worked out… two from a different moment)… by way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… nine… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… six… by way of dissociation condition… five… by way of presence condition… nine (abbreviated. It should be worked out the same as the pair with applied thought)… By way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition… nine. ^7-35-162

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-210-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-210-1-0

Root: four, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: three, post-arising: three, repetition: nine, kamma: six, result: nine, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: nine, path: four, association: six, dissociation: five, presence: nine, absence: nine, disappearance: nine, non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-35-163

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-210-2-0

A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. A state with rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. A state with rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-35-164

A state without rapture is a condition for a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. A state without rapture is a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (3) ^7-35-165

A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition. A state with rapture and a state without rapture are a condition for a state with rapture and a state without rapture by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. (3)
(The negative exposition and the counting are also the same as in the pair with applied thought. Even if it does not agree, one should count after reviewing this positive method; the other two counts should be counted.)
The With-Rapture Pair is finished. ^7-35-166

###### The Accompanied-by-Rapture Pair ^7-4-1-1-211-0

####### *1-7. The Dependence Section and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-211-1-0

Dependent on a state accompanied by rapture, a state accompanied by rapture arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by rapture, three aggregates… two aggregates… (in this way the pair accompanied by rapture should be expanded, the same as the pair with rapture, without difference; the reference is without difference.)
The Accompanied-by-Rapture Pair is finished. ^7-35-167

###### The Accompanied-by-Happiness Pair ^7-4-1-1-212-0

###### 1-6. The Dependence Section and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-213-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-214-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-214-1-0

Dependent on a state accompanied by happiness, a state accompanied by happiness arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by happiness, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates, one aggregate; at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state accompanied by happiness, a state not accompanied by happiness arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by happiness, happiness and matter produced by consciousness. (In this way the pair accompanied by happiness should be expanded, as in the positive Dependence Section of the pair with rapture.)
Root: nine, object: nine… pre-arising: six, repetition: six, kamma: nine… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-35-168

Dependent on a state accompanied by happiness, a state accompanied by happiness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by happiness and without roots, two aggregates; dependent on two aggregates… (the base should be worked out). Dependent on the aggregates accompanied by happiness and without roots, happiness and matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a state accompanied by happiness, a state accompanied by happiness and a state not accompanied by happiness arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate accompanied by happiness and without roots, two aggregates, happiness and matter produced by consciousness, two aggregates…. (3)
Dependent on a state not accompanied by happiness, a state not accompanied by happiness arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not accompanied by happiness and without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… dependent on happiness without roots, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate not accompanied by happiness, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element…. (As far as non-percipient beings) dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (As in the pair with rapture, the same as the not-root condition, without difference; everywhere nine questions.)
Not-root: nine, not-object: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: six, not-path: nine, not-association: three, not-dissociation: six, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three.
The negative method.
(In the same way, the other two counts and the Co-arising Section are the same as the Dependence Section. In the Condition Section, both the course of life and rebirth should be expanded; as in the negative Condition Section of the pair with rapture, in the course of life the physical base should also be expanded; as in the pair with rapture there is only one delusion; in the same way, the other two counts, the Support Section, the Conjoined Section and the Association Section should be done as in the pair with rapture.) ^7-35-169

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-215-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-216-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-217-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-217-1-0

A state accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of root condition… four. (The object and predominance conditions too are the same as in the pair with rapture; the difference is 'happiness'.) ^7-35-170

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-217-2-0

A state accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by happiness. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following happiness. Death-consciousness accompanied by happiness for rebirth-consciousness not accompanied by happiness… life-continuum accompanied by happiness for adverting… body-consciousness accompanied by happiness for the resultant mind element… the resultant mind-consciousness element accompanied by happiness for the functional mind-consciousness element… life-continuum accompanied by happiness for life-continuum not accompanied by happiness… the wholesome and unwholesome accompanied by happiness for emergence not accompanied by happiness… the functional for emergence… fruit is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by happiness and for happiness. (1)
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state not accompanied by happiness by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not accompanied by happiness…. (The base. All three are the same as the pair with rapture.) ^7-35-171

A state accompanied by happiness and a state not accompanied by happiness are a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness and happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by happiness. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness and happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following happiness; death-consciousness accompanied by happiness and happiness for rebirth-consciousness not accompanied by happiness, life-continuum accompanied by happiness and happiness for adverting… body-consciousness accompanied by happiness and happiness for the resultant mind element… the resultant mind-consciousness element accompanied by happiness and happiness for the functional mind-consciousness element… life-continuum accompanied by happiness and happiness for life-continuum not accompanied by happiness… the wholesome and unwholesome accompanied by happiness and happiness for emergence not accompanied by happiness… the functional for emergence… fruit is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by happiness and happiness are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by happiness and for happiness. (3)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of mutuality condition… by way of support condition. ^7-35-172

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-217-3-0

A state accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of strong-support condition… three.
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state not accompanied by happiness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not accompanied by happiness, with consciousness not accompanied by happiness one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not accompanied by happiness… on wisdom… on lust… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on temperature… on food… on lodging, and on happiness, with consciousness not accompanied by happiness one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… causes a split in the monastic community; faith not accompanied by happiness… lodging and happiness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not accompanied by happiness… for wisdom… for lust… for hatred… for longing… for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain, for the path, for the attainment of fruition and for happiness. (1)
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not accompanied by happiness, with consciousness accompanied by happiness one gives a gift… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not accompanied by happiness… on wisdom… on lust… on longing… on bodily pleasure… on bodily pain… on temperature… on food… on lodging, and on happiness, with consciousness accompanied by happiness one gives a gift… produces an attainment; with consciousness accompanied by happiness one takes what is not given; tells lies… speaks divisively… chatters idly… breaks into houses… carries off plunder… robs a single house… lies in ambush on the road… goes to another's wife… raids a village… raids a town; faith not accompanied by happiness… lodging and happiness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith accompanied by happiness… for wisdom… for lust… for conceit… for wrong view… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for the path, for the attainment of fruition. (2)
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness and a state not accompanied by happiness by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not accompanied by happiness, with consciousness accompanied by happiness one gives a gift… (the same as the second course) fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not accompanied by happiness… on lodging and on happiness, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, with consciousness accompanied by happiness one takes what is not given…; faith not accompanied by happiness… lodging and happiness are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for what is accompanied by happiness… for faith… for longing… for bodily pleasure, for the path, for the attainment of fruition and for happiness. (3)
A state accompanied by happiness and a state not accompanied by happiness are a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of strong-support condition… three. ^7-35-173

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-217-4-0

A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state not accompanied by happiness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness not accompanied by happiness, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust not accompanied by happiness arises… distress arises, happiness arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates not accompanied by happiness and for happiness. (1)
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness accompanied by happiness, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust accompanied by happiness arises, wrong view arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates accompanied by happiness. (2)
A state not accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness and a state not accompanied by happiness by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, with consciousness accompanied by happiness, as impermanent… one sees it with insight, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, happiness and the associated aggregates arise. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates accompanied by happiness and for happiness. (3)
By way of post-arising condition… three… by way of repetition condition… nine. ^7-35-174

####### *The Kamma Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-217-5-0

A state accompanied by happiness is a condition for a state accompanied by happiness by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. (Six kamma: four, both the co-arising and the from-a-different-moment are to be worked out; two from a different moment.)… By way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… nine… by way of jhāna condition… nine… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… six… by way of dissociation condition… five… by way of presence condition… nine… by way of absence condition… nine… by way of disappearance condition… nine… by way of non-disappearance condition… nine. ^7-35-175

Root: four, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: three, post-arising: three, repetition: nine, kamma: six, result: nine, nutriment: four, faculty: nine, jhāna: nine, path: four, association: six, dissociation: five, presence: nine, absence: nine, disappearance: nine, non-disappearance: nine.

(3)

Dependent on a state that is not accompanied by equanimity, a state accompanied by equanimity arises not because of the root condition – dependent on equanimity without roots, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on equanimity, the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates accompanied by equanimity; dependent on equanimity accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness.
Dependent on a state that is not accompanied by equanimity, states accompanied by equanimity and states that are not accompanied by equanimity arise not because of the root condition – dependent on equanimity without roots, the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, dependent on equanimity without roots, the associated aggregates, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on equanimity, the associated aggregates and matter produced by kamma, at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on equanimity, the associated aggregates, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma, at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates accompanied by equanimity, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma, at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on the physical base, equanimity and the associated aggregates. Dependent on a state accompanied by equanimity and a state that is not accompanied by equanimity, states accompanied by equanimity and states that are not accompanied by equanimity arise not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots accompanied by equanimity and equanimity, two aggregates and matter produced by consciousness, dependent on two aggregates… dependent on one aggregate without roots accompanied by equanimity and equanimity, two aggregates, dependent on two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates without roots accompanied by equanimity, equanimity and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate accompanied by equanimity and equanimity, two aggregates and matter produced by kamma, dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate accompanied by equanimity and equanimity, two aggregates, dependent on two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates without roots accompanied by equanimity, equanimity and the great elements, matter produced by kamma; at the moment of rebirth without roots, dependent on one aggregate accompanied by equanimity and the physical base, two aggregates and equanimity, dependent on two aggregates… (abbreviated). ####### *The Root Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-239-1-0

A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of root condition – roots accompanied by equanimity are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (In this way four questions, as for the With-Applied-Thought Pair.)
A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of object condition… by way of predominance condition. (As with the With-Rapture Pair, so the object condition and the predominance condition should be expanded; the difference is "equanimity".) ^7-35-186

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-239-2-0

A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity. (1)
A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for each following equanimity; death-consciousness accompanied by equanimity for rebirth-consciousness not accompanied by equanimity… adverting for the aggregates not accompanied by equanimity… the resultant mind element for the resultant mind-consciousness element not accompanied by equanimity… life-continuum accompanied by equanimity for life-continuum not accompanied by equanimity… the wholesome and unwholesome accompanied by equanimity for emergence not accompanied by equanimity… the functional for emergence… fruit for emergence… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition not accompanied by equanimity. (2)
A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity and a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity and for equanimity. (3) ^7-35-187

A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding equanimity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for each following equanimity; preceding aggregates not accompanied by equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not accompanied by equanimity; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding equanimity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity; death-consciousness not accompanied by equanimity for rebirth-consciousness accompanied by equanimity… life-continuum not accompanied by equanimity for adverting… the body-consciousness element for the resultant mind element… the resultant mind-consciousness element not accompanied by equanimity for the functional mind-consciousness element… life-continuum not accompanied by equanimity for life-continuum accompanied by equanimity… the wholesome and unwholesome not accompanied by equanimity for emergence accompanied by equanimity… the functional for emergence… fruit is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2)
A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity and a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding equanimity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity and for equanimity. (3) ^7-35-188

States accompanied by equanimity and states that are not accompanied by equanimity are a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity and equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity and equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for each following equanimity; death-consciousness accompanied by equanimity and equanimity for rebirth-consciousness not accompanied by equanimity… adverting and equanimity for the aggregates not accompanied by equanimity… the resultant mind element and equanimity for the resultant mind-consciousness element not accompanied by equanimity… life-continuum accompanied by equanimity and equanimity for life-continuum not accompanied by equanimity… the wholesome and unwholesome accompanied by equanimity and equanimity for emergence not accompanied by equanimity… the functional and equanimity for emergence… fruit and equanimity for emergence… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception and equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition not accompanied by equanimity. (The base should be worked out.) Preceding aggregates accompanied by equanimity and equanimity are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates accompanied by equanimity and for equanimity. (3)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… nine… by way of mutuality condition… nine… by way of support condition… nine. ^7-35-189

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-239-3-0

A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of strong-support condition… three.
A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not accompanied by equanimity, with consciousness not accompanied by equanimity one gives a gift, the precepts… the observance-day duties… jhāna not accompanied by equanimity… insight… the path… one produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not accompanied by equanimity… wisdom… lust… hatred… delusion… conceit… wrong view… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging and equanimity, with consciousness not accompanied by equanimity one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith not accompanied by equanimity… lodging and equanimity are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not accompanied by equanimity… for wisdom… for lust… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path, for the attainment of fruition and for equanimity. (1)
A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of strong-support condition (all three kinds of strong-support). Relying on faith not accompanied by equanimity, with consciousness accompanied by equanimity one gives a gift… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not accompanied by equanimity… lodging and equanimity, with consciousness accompanied by equanimity one gives a gift… produces an attainment, takes what is not given, tells lies, speaks divisively… chatters idly… breaks into houses… carries off plunder… robs a single house… lies in ambush on the road… goes to another's wife… raids a village… raids a town; faith not accompanied by equanimity… lodging and equanimity are a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith accompanied by equanimity… for longing… for the path, for the attainment of fruition.
A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity and a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of strong-support condition (three kinds of strong-support, the same as the second pass). (3)
States accompanied by equanimity and states that are not accompanied by equanimity are a condition for a state accompanied by equanimity by way of strong-support condition… three. ^7-35-190

####### *The Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-239-4-0

A state that is not accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of pre-arising condition… three (the same as the With-Rapture Pair). ^7-35-191

####### *The Post-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-239-5-0

A state accompanied by equanimity is a condition for a state that is not accompanied by equanimity by way of post-arising condition… three… by way of repetition condition… nine… by way of kamma condition… six. (Four co-arising ones and those from a different moment should be worked out, and two from a different moment.)… By way of result condition… nine… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… nine… by way of jhāna condition… nine… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… six… by way of dissociation condition… five… by way of presence condition… nine… by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. (These conditions should be divided up as in the With-Rapture Pair.) ^7-35-192

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-240-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-241-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-241-1-0

Root: four, object: nine, predominance: nine, proximity: nine, contiguity: nine, co-arising: nine, mutuality: nine, support: nine, strong-support: nine, pre-arising: three, post-arising: three, repetition: nine, kamma: six, result: nine, nutriment: four, faculty: nine, jhāna: nine, path: four, association: six, dissociation: five, presence: nine, absence: nine, disappearance: nine, non-disappearance: nine.
(In this way the exposition of the negative method and the other three counts should also be worked out like the With-Rapture Pair.)
The Accompanied-by-Equanimity Pair is finished. ^7-35-193

###### The Sense-Sphere Pair ^7-4-1-1-242-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-243-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-244-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-245-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-245-1-0

Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate of the sense sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… one great element… (abbreviated). Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates not of the sense sphere. Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the physical base, the aggregates not of the sense sphere, dependent on the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) ^7-35-194

Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere, matter produced by kamma. Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-35-195

Dependent on a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth, dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. Dependent on a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and…. Dependent on a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise because of the root condition – at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates and… dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements, matter produced by kamma. (3) (Abbreviated.) ^7-35-196

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-246-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-247-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-247-1-0

Root: nine, object: four, predominance: five, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: nine, mutuality: six, support: nine, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: nine, result: nine, nutriment: nine, faculty: nine, jhāna: nine, path: nine, association: four, dissociation: nine, presence: nine, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: nine. ^7-35-197

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-248-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-249-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-249-1-0

Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate of the sense sphere without roots, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… (up to non-percipient beings) dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Not because of the object condition… three. ^7-35-198

####### *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-249-2-0

Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition… three.
Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere, a predominant state not of the sense sphere, dependent on one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the resultant aggregates not of the sense sphere, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
Dependent on a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the resultant aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (the other two are as usual) not because of the proximity condition… not because of the pre-arising condition… not because of the post-arising condition. ^7-35-199

####### *The Not-Repetition Condition* ^7-4-1-1-249-3-0

Dependent on a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the repetition condition… three.
Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on the aggregates not of the sense sphere, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (the base should be worked out) dependent on one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… dependent on two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
(The remaining three are as usual. Abbreviated.) ^7-35-200

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-250-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-250-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: two, not-result: five, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-201

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-4-1-1-251-0

(The remaining counts and the Co-arising Section should also be worked out.) ^7-U443

###### The Sense-Sphere Pair ^7-4-1-1-252-0

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-253-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-254-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-254-1-0

With a state of the sense sphere as condition, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate of the sense sphere as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… with two aggregates as condition… at the moment of rebirth… (up to the internal great elements) with the physical base as condition, the aggregates of the sense sphere. With a state of the sense sphere as condition, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not of the sense sphere; at the moment of rebirth…. With a state of the sense sphere as condition, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, the aggregates not of the sense sphere, with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
With a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition (the same as the Dependence Section). With a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition and… at the moment of rebirth…. With a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition and… with the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) (Abbreviated.)
Root: nine, object: four, predominance: nine, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: nine, mutuality: six, support: nine, strong-support: four, pre-arising: four, repetition: four, kamma: nine… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-35-202

####### *The Not-Root Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-254-2-0

With a state of the sense sphere as condition, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate of the sense sphere without roots as condition… at the moment of rebirth without roots… (up to non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness, with the physical base as condition, the aggregates of the sense sphere without roots, with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Not because of the object condition… three. ^7-35-203

####### *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-254-3-0

With a state of the sense sphere as condition, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition… one (up to non-percipient beings). With a state of the sense sphere as condition, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, a predominant state not of the sense sphere, with the physical base as condition, the resultant aggregates not of the sense sphere; at the moment of rebirth…. With a state of the sense sphere as condition, states of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere arise not because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, the resultant aggregates not of the sense sphere, with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
With a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere as condition, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the predominance condition (the same as the Dependence Section. The base should be worked out.) With the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the physical base as condition, a predominant state not of the sense sphere, with one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition and… at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) With one resultant aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… with two aggregates as condition and… with the resultant aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
Not because of the strong-support condition… three… not because of the repetition condition (in the pure and in the immaterial-mixed cases "result" should be specified; in the material-mixed cases there is none. Abbreviated). ^7-35-204

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-254-4-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three.
The negative method. ^7-U444

###### The Support Section ^7-4-1-1-255-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out.) ^7-U445

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-256-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-257-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-257-1-0

Conjoined with a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate of the sense sphere, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state that is not of the sense sphere, a state that is not of the sense sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not of the sense sphere, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Root: two, object: two, predominance: two (two in every case), non-disappearance: two.
The positive method.
Conjoined with a state of the sense sphere, a state of the sense sphere arises not because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate of the sense sphere without roots, three aggregates… conjoined with two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… conjoined with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness.
Not-root: one, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: two.
The negative method. ^7-35-205

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-258-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U446

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-259-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-260-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-261-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-261-1-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of root condition – roots of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of root condition – roots not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of root condition – roots not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) ^7-35-206

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-261-2-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of object condition – after giving a gift, the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties… one reviews good deeds done in the past, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust… distress arises; noble ones review change-of-lineage, they review cleansing; abandoned defilements… suppressed defilements… good deeds done in the past… one sees the eye… the physical base and the aggregates of the sense sphere with insight as impermanent… distress arises; the visible-form base is a condition, by way of object condition, for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness. (1)
A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of object condition – with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound, with knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness of the sense sphere; the aggregates of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future. (2) ^7-35-207

A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of object condition – nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for the path and for fruit; with knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness not of the sense sphere; the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the aggregates not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future. (1)
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of object condition – after emerging from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view arises… noble ones, after emerging from the path, review the path; fruit… they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing and for adverting; one reviews the base of infinite space, reviews the base of infinite consciousness, reviews the base of nothingness, reviews the base of neither perception nor non-perception, reviews the divine eye, reviews the divine ear element, reviews knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds… knowledge of recollecting past lives… knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… reviews knowledge of the future; one sees the aggregates not of the sense sphere with insight as impermanent… distress arises. (2) ^7-35-208

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-261-3-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – a gift… the precepts… the observance-day duties… good deeds done in the past: giving them weight, one reviews them, enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; trainees, giving it weight, review change-of-lineage; giving it weight, they review cleansing; the eye… the physical base and the aggregates of the sense sphere: giving them weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – a predominant state of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-35-209

A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the path and for fruit. Co-arising predominance – a predominant state not of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – after emerging from jhāna, giving the jhāna weight one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; noble ones, after emerging from the path, giving the path weight, review it; fruit… giving nibbāna weight, they review it; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage and for cleansing; giving the base of infinite space weight, one reviews it, the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness… giving the base of neither perception nor non-perception weight, one reviews it, the divine eye… the divine ear element… knowledge of supernormal powers… giving knowledge of the future weight, one reviews it; giving the aggregates not of the sense sphere weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – a predominant state not of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness; a state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – a predominant state not of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-210

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-261-4-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates of the sense sphere; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… adverting for the aggregates of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of proximity condition.
A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness of the sense sphere for rebirth-consciousness not of the sense sphere… adverting for the aggregates not of the sense sphere is a condition, by way of proximity condition; the aggregates of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence not of the sense sphere; the preliminary work for the first jhāna is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the first jhāna… for the fourth jhāna… the preliminary work for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… for the divine eye… for the divine ear element… for knowledge of supernormal powers… for knowledge of others' minds… for knowledge of recollecting past lives… for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… the preliminary work for knowledge of the future is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for knowledge of the future; change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing for the path… conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-211

A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not of the sense sphere; the path for fruit, fruit for fruit, for one emerging from cessation the base of neither perception nor non-perception for the attainment of fruition is a condition, by way of proximity condition. A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness not of the sense sphere for rebirth-consciousness of the sense sphere, life-continuum not of the sense sphere for adverting, the aggregates not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence of the sense sphere…. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… seven… by way of mutuality condition… six… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-212

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-261-5-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith of the sense sphere, one gives a gift… the precepts… the observance-day duties… produces insight, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue of the sense sphere… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, one gives a gift… produces insight, kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith of the sense sphere… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith of the sense sphere… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain. (1)
A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith of the sense sphere, one produces jhāna not of the sense sphere, the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; relying on virtue of the sense sphere… lodging, one produces jhāna not of the sense sphere, the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; faith of the sense sphere… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the sense sphere… for wisdom… for the path, for the attainment of fruition; the preliminary work for the first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the first jhāna… for the fourth jhāna… for the base of infinite space… for the first path… the preliminary work for the fourth path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. (2) ^7-35-213

A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not of the sense sphere, one produces jhāna, the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; relying on virtue not of the sense sphere… wisdom, one produces jhāna not of the sense sphere… the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; faith not of the sense sphere… wisdom is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the sense sphere… for wisdom, for the path, for the attainment of fruition; the first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second jhāna… the third jhāna… the fourth jhāna… the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception; the first path for the second path… the second path for the third path… the third path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path; the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not of the sense sphere, one gives a gift, the precepts… the observance-day duties… produces insight, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not of the sense sphere… wisdom, one gives a gift, the precepts… the observance-day duties… produces insight, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; faith not of the sense sphere… wisdom is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith of the sense sphere… for wisdom, for lust… for longing, for bodily pleasure, for bodily pain; relying on the path, noble ones see formations with insight as impermanent…; the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for noble ones' analytical knowledge of meaning… analytical knowledge of teachings… analytical knowledge of language… analytical knowledge of ready expression… for skill in what is possible and what is impossible; the attainment of fruition is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for bodily pleasure. (2) ^7-35-214

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-261-6-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one sees the physical base with insight as impermanent… distress arises; the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates of the sense sphere. A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – with the divine eye one sees visible form, with the divine ear element one hears sound. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates not of the sense sphere. (2)
By way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… three. ^7-35-215

####### *The Kamma Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-261-7-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment (abbreviated).
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment (abbreviated). A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment (abbreviated). A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment (abbreviated). (3)
By way of result condition… four… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two. ^7-35-216

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-4-1-1-261-8-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising (abbreviated) – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates not of the sense sphere. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for the aggregates not of the sense sphere. (2)
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising. Co-arising – the aggregates not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – the aggregates not of the sense sphere are a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for this body that arose earlier. (1) ^7-35-217

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-261-9-0

A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates not of the sense sphere. Pre-arising – with the divine eye one sees visible form… (the same as pre-arising). (2)
A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising (abbreviated). A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (the same as dissociation). A state that is not of the sense sphere is a condition for a state of the sense sphere and a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). (3) ^7-35-218

States of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere are a condition for a state of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – the aggregates not of the sense sphere and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – the aggregates not of the sense sphere and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. States of the sense sphere and states that are not of the sense sphere are a condition for a state that is not of the sense sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate not of the sense sphere and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. By way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. (2) ^7-35-219

With a state not of the fine-material sphere as condition, a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere arise not because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, resultant aggregates of the fine-material sphere; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth….
