With a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere as condition, a state of the fine-material sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – with the aggregates of the fine-material sphere and the physical base as condition, predominance of the fine-material sphere; with one resultant aggregate of the fine-material sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth, with one aggregate of the fine-material sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and…. With a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere as condition, a state not of the fine-material sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – with the resultant aggregates of the fine-material sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. With a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere as condition, a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere arise not because of the predominance condition – with one resultant aggregate of the fine-material sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and… with the resultant aggregates of the fine-material sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3) (Abbreviated.) ^7-35-242

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-285-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-286-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-286-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: nine, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine (in the pure case, in the immaterial and in the mixed, "result" should be specified), not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: one, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-243

###### The Support Section ^7-4-1-1-287-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out.) ^7-U448

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-288-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-289-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-289-1-0

Conjoined with a state of the fine-material sphere, a state of the fine-material sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate of the fine-material sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state not of the fine-material sphere, a state not of the fine-material sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not of the fine-material sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Root: two, object: two (two in every case), non-disappearance: two.
The positive method.
Conjoined with a state not of the fine-material sphere, a state not of the fine-material sphere arises not because of the root condition (abbreviated).
Not-root: one, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: one.
The negative method. ^7-35-244

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-290-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U449

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-291-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-292-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-293-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-293-1-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of root condition – roots of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (3)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of root condition – roots not of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-245

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-293-2-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition – by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness of the fine-material sphere; aggregates of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma and knowledge of the future. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition – one reviews the first jhāna… reviews the fourth jhāna, reviews the divine eye, reviews the divine ear element… knowledge of supernormal powers… knowledge of others' minds… knowledge of recollecting past lives… knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… reviews knowledge of the future; the aggregates of the fine-material sphere as impermanent… distress arises. (2) ^7-35-246

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition – after giving a gift, the precepts… the observance-day duties… one reviews good deeds done in the past, enjoys and delights in them; with that as object, lust… distress arises; noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, review fruit, review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path, fruit and adverting; noble ones review abandoned defilements… suppressed defilements… previously… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates not of the fine-material sphere as impermanent… distress arises; the base of infinite space for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of object condition, for body-consciousness. (1)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition – one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element, by knowledge of others' minds knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness not of the fine-material sphere; aggregates not of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma and knowledge of the future. (2) ^7-35-247

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-293-3-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – one reviews the first jhāna, giving it weight, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises… the fourth jhāna… the divine eye… the divine ear element… knowledge of supernormal powers… knowledge of others' minds… knowledge of recollecting past lives… knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… knowledge of the future, giving it weight, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving the aggregates of the fine-material sphere weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-248

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… the observance-day duties… giving good deeds done in the past weight, one reviews them, enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; noble ones, having emerged from the path, giving the path weight, review it… fruit… giving nibbāna weight, review it; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path and fruit; the eye… giving the physical base and the aggregates not of the fine-material sphere weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; giving the base of infinite space weight, one reviews it… giving the base of neither perception nor non-perception weight, one reviews it. Co-arising predominance – predominance not of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-35-249

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-293-4-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates of the fine-material sphere. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for rebirth-consciousness not of the fine-material sphere; life-continuum of the fine-material sphere, for adverting… aggregates of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence not of the fine-material sphere. (2) ^7-35-250

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not of the fine-material sphere; conformity for change-of-lineage… the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness not of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for rebirth-consciousness of the fine-material sphere; aggregates not of the fine-material sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence of the fine-material sphere; the preliminary work for the first jhāna is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the first jhāna… the fourth jhāna… the divine eye… the divine ear element… knowledge of supernormal powers… knowledge of others' minds… knowledge of recollecting past lives… knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… knowledge of the future; the preliminary work for knowledge of the future is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for knowledge of the future. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… seven… by way of mutuality condition… six… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-251

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-293-5-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith of the fine-material sphere, one produces jhāna of the fine-material sphere, produces higher knowledge… produces an attainment; relying on virtue of the fine-material sphere… wisdom, one produces jhāna of the fine-material sphere… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; faith of the fine-material sphere… wisdom of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith of the fine-material sphere… wisdom; the first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second jhāna; the second jhāna, for the third jhāna… the third jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth jhāna. (1)
A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith of the fine-material sphere, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties; jhāna of the fine-material sphere… insight… the path… one produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue of the fine-material sphere… wisdom, one gives a gift… fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; faith of the fine-material sphere… wisdom of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the fine-material sphere… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… the path, the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-252

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not of the fine-material sphere, one gives a gift… undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties; jhāna not of the fine-material sphere… insight… the path… one produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not of the fine-material sphere… wisdom, lust… food, lodging, one gives a gift… kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith not of the fine-material sphere… wisdom… lust… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the fine-material sphere… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… the path, the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not of the fine-material sphere, one produces jhāna of the fine-material sphere… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; relying on virtue not of the fine-material sphere… lodging, one produces jhāna of the fine-material sphere… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; faith not of the fine-material sphere… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith of the fine-material sphere… wisdom; the preliminary work for the first jhāna is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the first jhāna… the fourth jhāna… the divine eye, the preliminary work… the divine ear element… knowledge of supernormal powers… knowledge of others' minds… knowledge of recollecting past lives… knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma… the preliminary work for knowledge of the future is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for knowledge of the future. (2) ^7-35-253

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-293-6-0

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for body-consciousness; the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates not of the fine-material sphere. A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates of the fine-material sphere. (2)
By way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… three. ^7-35-254

####### *The Kamma Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-293-7-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates of the fine-material sphere. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by kamma. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates of the fine-material sphere and matter produced by kamma. (3)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition not of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. From a different moment – volition not of the fine-material sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for resultant aggregates not of the fine-material sphere and matter produced by kamma…. (1)
By way of result condition… four… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two. ^7-35-255

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-4-1-1-293-8-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for aggregates of the fine-material sphere. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for aggregates of the fine-material sphere. (2) ^7-35-256

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-293-9-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition (the same as the Dependence Section). (3)
A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – at the moment of rebirth the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for aggregates of the fine-material sphere. Pre-arising – one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element; the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for aggregates of the fine-material sphere. (2) ^7-35-257

A state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere are a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate of the fine-material sphere and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and… at the moment of rebirth…. A state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere are a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – aggregates of the fine-material sphere and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. Post-arising – aggregates of the fine-material sphere and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body that arose earlier. Post-arising – aggregates of the fine-material sphere and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
By way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-35-258

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-294-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-295-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-295-1-0

Root: four, object: four, predominance: four, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: seven, mutuality: six, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: three, kamma: four, result: four, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: seven. ^7-35-259

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-295-2-0

A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition…. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. A state of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of co-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. (3) ^7-35-260

A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state not of the fine-material sphere is a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere are a condition for a state of the fine-material sphere – co-arising, pre-arising. A state of the fine-material sphere and a state not of the fine-material sphere are a condition for a state not of the fine-material sphere – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-35-261

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-297-0

Not-root: seven, not-object: seven, not-predominance: seven, not-proximity: seven, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: six, not-mutuality: six, not-support: six, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: seven, not-post-arising: seven… not-association: six, not-dissociation: five, not-presence: five, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: five. ^7-35-262

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-298-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: four, not-proximity, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four… not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-263

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-299-0

With not-root as condition – object: four, predominance: four (the positive list of headings should be worked out)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Fine-Material-Sphere Pair is finished. ^7-35-264

###### The Immaterial-Sphere Pair ^7-4-1-1-300-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-301-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-302-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-303-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-303-1-0

Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates…. (3) ^7-35-265

Dependent on a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth, dependent on one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by kamma… two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element…. (1)
Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) (Abbreviated.) ^7-35-266

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-304-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-304-1-0

Root: five, object: two, predominance: five, proximity: two, contiguity: two, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: five, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: five, result: two, nutriment: five… non-disappearance: five. ^7-35-267

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-305-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-306-0

####### *The Not-Root and Not-Object Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-306-1-0

Dependent on a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots and not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… (as far as non-percipient beings) dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) Not because of the object condition… three. ^7-35-268

####### *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-306-2-0

Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere, predominance of the immaterial sphere; dependent on one resultant aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Dependent on a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1) Not because of the proximity condition… not because of the strong-support condition. ^7-35-269

####### *The Not-Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-306-3-0

Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates…. (The base should be worked out.) Dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) Not because of the post-arising condition…. ^7-35-270

####### *The Not-Repetition Condition* ^7-4-1-1-306-4-0

Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on one resultant aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the repetition condition – dependent on the aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) (Abbreviated.) ^7-35-271

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-307-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-307-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: two, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: four, not-kamma: two, not-result: five, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-272

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-4-1-1-308-0

(In the same way, the other two counts and the Co-arising Section should be worked out.) ^7-U450

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-309-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-310-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-311-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-311-1-0

With a state of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere as condition… (as far as the internal great elements) with the physical base as condition, aggregates not of the immaterial sphere. With a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, aggregates of the immaterial sphere. With a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, aggregates of the immaterial sphere; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-273

With a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate of the immaterial sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and…. With a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. With a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate of the immaterial sphere and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and… with the aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-274

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-312-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-312-1-0

Root: nine, object: four, predominance: nine, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: nine, mutuality: four, support: nine, strong-support: four, pre-arising: four, repetition: four, kamma: nine, result: two… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-35-275

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-313-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-314-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-314-1-0

With a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate without roots and not of the immaterial sphere as condition… (as far as non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates without roots and not of the immaterial sphere; with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) Not because of the object condition… three.
With a state of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – with the aggregates of the immaterial sphere as condition, predominance of the immaterial sphere; with one resultant aggregate of the immaterial sphere as condition… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
With a state not of the immaterial sphere as condition, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises not because of the predominance condition – with one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere as condition… at the moment of rebirth… (as far as non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates not of the immaterial sphere. (1) ^7-35-276

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-315-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-315-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: four, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity, not-mutuality, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: four, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-277

###### The Support Section ^7-4-1-1-316-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out.) ^7-U451

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-317-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-318-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-318-1-0

Conjoined with a state of the immaterial sphere, a state of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Conjoined with a state not of the immaterial sphere, a state not of the immaterial sphere arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not of the immaterial sphere, three aggregates… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Root: two… non-disappearance: two (the positive method).
Not-root: one, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: two (the negative method). ^7-35-278

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-319-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U452

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-320-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-321-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-322-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-1-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of root condition – roots of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates; at the moment of rebirth…. (The base should be worked out.) Roots of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base should be worked out.) Roots of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of root condition – roots not of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-279

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-2-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition – the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of object condition, for the base of infinite consciousness; the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of object condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition – one reviews the base of infinite space, the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness… reviews the base of neither perception nor non-perception; the aggregates of the immaterial sphere as impermanent… distress arises; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness of the immaterial sphere; aggregates of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, knowledge of the future and adverting. (2)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust… distress arises; good deeds done in the past… from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, review fruit, review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path, fruit and adverting; noble ones review abandoned defilements, suppressed defilements… previously… the eye… the physical base, the aggregates not of the immaterial sphere as impermanent… distress arises, one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element, by knowledge of others' minds knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness not of the immaterial sphere; the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… aggregates not of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, knowledge of others' minds, knowledge of recollecting past lives, knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma knowledge of the future and adverting. (1) ^7-35-280

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-3-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – one reviews the base of infinite space, giving it weight… reviews the base of neither perception nor non-perception, giving it weight; giving the aggregates of the immaterial sphere weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base should be worked out.) Predominance of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-281

A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving… virtue… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; giving it weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, giving the path weight, review it; fruit… giving nibbāna weight, they review it; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, cleansing, the path and fruit; the eye… giving the physical base and the aggregates not of the immaterial sphere weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving them weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-35-282

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-322-4-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates of the immaterial sphere. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness of the immaterial sphere for rebirth-consciousness not of the immaterial sphere, life-continuum of the immaterial sphere for adverting, aggregates of the immaterial sphere for emergence not of the immaterial sphere, and, for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception for the attainment of fruition are a condition, by way of proximity condition. (2) ^7-35-283

A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not of the immaterial sphere; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of proximity condition – death-consciousness not of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for rebirth-consciousness of the immaterial sphere; aggregates not of the immaterial sphere are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence of the immaterial sphere; the preliminary work for the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the base of infinite space; for the base of infinite consciousness… for the base of nothingness… the preliminary work for the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-284

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-5-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite consciousness; the base of infinite consciousness for the base of nothingness… the base of nothingness is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith of the immaterial sphere, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna not of the immaterial sphere, insight… the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment, fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue of the immaterial sphere… wisdom, one gives a gift… produces an attainment… fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; faith of the immaterial sphere… wisdom of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the immaterial sphere… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… the path, the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-285

A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not of the immaterial sphere, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna not of the immaterial sphere, insight… the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment, fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not of the immaterial sphere… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… temperature… food… lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment… kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith not of the immaterial sphere… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not of the immaterial sphere… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain, the path, the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – the preliminary work for the base of infinite space is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of infinite space… the preliminary work for the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the base of neither perception nor non-perception. (2) ^7-35-286

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-322-6-0

A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base as impermanent… distress arises; one sees a visible form with the divine eye, hears a sound with the divine ear element, the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates not of the immaterial sphere. A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of pre-arising condition. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates of the immaterial sphere. (2)
By way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… three. ^7-35-287

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-7-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment…. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of kamma condition – volition of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base should be worked out.) Volition of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition not of the immaterial sphere is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and matter produced by consciousness (abbreviated). (1) ^7-35-288

####### *The Result Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-322-8-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of result condition (abbreviated).
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of result condition (abbreviated)… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two. ^7-35-289

####### *The Dissociation Condition* ^7-4-1-1-322-9-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). (1)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of dissociation condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of dissociation condition. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of dissociation condition, for aggregates of the immaterial sphere. (2) ^7-35-290

####### *The Presence Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-322-10-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising…. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising…. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising (abbreviated). (3)
A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition. Pre-arising – the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for aggregates of the immaterial sphere. (2) ^7-35-291

A state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere are a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising. Co-arising – one aggregate of the immaterial sphere and the physical base are a condition, by way of presence condition, for three aggregates… two aggregates and…. A state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere are a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates of the immaterial sphere and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – aggregates of the immaterial sphere and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2)
By way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition. ^7-35-292

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-323-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-323-1-0

Root: four, object: three, predominance: four, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: three, kamma: four, result: two, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: seven. ^7-35-293

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-323-2-0

A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition. A state of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of co-arising condition. (3) ^7-35-294

A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state not of the immaterial sphere is a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere are a condition for a state of the immaterial sphere – co-arising, pre-arising. A state of the immaterial sphere and a state not of the immaterial sphere are a condition for a state not of the immaterial sphere – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-35-295

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-324-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-324-1-0

Not-root: seven, not-object: seven, not-predominance: seven, not-proximity: seven, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: five, not-mutuality: five, not-support: five, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: six, not-post-arising: seven… not-association: five, not-dissociation: four, not-presence: four, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-35-296

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-325-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: four, not-proximity, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four… not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-297

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-326-0

With not-root as condition – object: three, predominance: four (the positive list of headings should be worked out)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Immaterial-Sphere Pair is finished. ^7-35-298

###### The Included Pair ^7-4-1-1-327-0

###### 1-7. The Dependence Section and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-328-0

Dependent on a state included, a state included arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate included, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth…. (Just as the mundane pair in the Minor Intermediate Pairs, so this pair too should be worked out, without difference.)
The Included Pair is finished. ^7-35-299

###### The Leading-Out Pair ^7-4-1-1-329-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-330-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-331-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-332-0

Dependent on a state leading out, a state leading out arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate leading out, three aggregates… two aggregates…. Dependent on a state leading out, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates leading out, matter produced by consciousness. Dependent on a state leading out, a state leading out and a state not leading out arise because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate leading out, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates…. (3)
Dependent on a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate not leading out, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element…. (1)
Dependent on a state leading out and a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates leading out and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-35-300

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-333-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-333-1-0

Root: five, object: two, predominance: five, proximity, contiguity: two, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: five, strong-support: two, pre-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: five, result: one, nutriment: five… non-disappearance: five. ^7-35-301

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-334-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-335-0

####### *The Not-Root and Not-Object Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-335-1-0

Dependent on a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots and not leading out, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth without roots… dependent on the aggregates, the physical base; dependent on the physical base, the aggregates; one great element… (as far as non-percipient beings) dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) Not because of the object condition… three. ^7-35-302

####### *The Not-Predominance Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-335-2-0

Dependent on a state leading out, a state leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on the aggregates leading out, predominance leading out. (1)
Dependent on a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – dependent on one aggregate not leading out, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… at the moment of rebirth… (as far as non-percipient beings, to be worked out). (1)
Not because of the proximity condition… not because of the contiguity condition… not because of the mutuality condition…. ^7-35-303

####### *The Not-Pre-arising Condition* ^7-4-1-1-335-3-0

Dependent on a state leading out, a state leading out arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate leading out…. Dependent on a state leading out, a state not leading out arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the aggregates leading out, matter produced by consciousness. (2)
Dependent on a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises not because of the pre-arising condition – in the immaterial realm, dependent on one aggregate not leading out, three aggregates… two aggregates… dependent on the aggregates not leading out, matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth… (as far as non-percipient beings). (1)
Dependent on a state leading out and a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises not because of the pre-arising condition – dependent on the aggregates leading out and the great elements, matter produced by consciousness. (1) ^7-35-304

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-336-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-336-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: two, not-proximity: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: one, not-kamma: two, not-result: five, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-305
