(In the same way, the other two counts and the Co-arising Section should be worked out.) ^7-U453

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-337-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-338-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-339-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-339-1-0

With a state leading out as condition, a state leading out arises because of the root condition… three (the same as the Dependence Section).
With a state not leading out as condition, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate not leading out as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… (as far as the internal great elements) with the physical base as condition, aggregates not leading out. With a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out arises because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, aggregates leading out. With a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out and a state not leading out arise because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, aggregates leading out; with the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-306

With a state leading out and a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate leading out and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and…. With a state leading out and a state not leading out as condition, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – with the aggregates leading out and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. With a state leading out and a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out and a state not leading out arise because of the root condition – with one aggregate leading out and the physical base as condition, three aggregates… two aggregates and… with the aggregates leading out and the great elements as condition, matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-307

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-340-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-340-1-0

Root: nine, object: four, predominance: nine, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: nine, mutuality: four, support: nine, strong-support: four, pre-arising: four, repetition: four, kamma: nine, result: one… non-disappearance: nine. ^7-35-308

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-341-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-342-0

####### *The Not-Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-342-1-0

With a state not leading out as condition, a state not leading out arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate without roots and not leading out as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness… two aggregates… (as far as non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates without roots and not leading out; with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1) ^7-35-309

####### *The Not-Object Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-342-2-0

With a state leading out as condition, a state not leading out arises not because of the object condition… three.
With a state leading out as condition, a state leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – with the aggregates leading out as condition, predominance leading out. (1)
With a state not leading out as condition, a state not leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – with one aggregate not leading out as condition… (as far as non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates not leading out. With a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – with the physical base as condition, predominance leading out. (2)
With a state leading out and a state not leading out as condition, a state leading out arises not because of the predominance condition – with the aggregates leading out and the physical base as condition, predominance leading out. (1) ^7-35-310

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-343-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-343-1-0

Not-root: one, not-object: three, not-predominance: four, not-proximity: three… not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: one, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three. ^7-35-311

###### The Support Section ^7-4-1-1-344-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out.) ^7-U454

###### The Conjoined Section ^7-4-1-1-345-0

####### *1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-345-1-0

Conjoined with a state leading out, a state leading out arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate leading out, three aggregates…; conjoined with two aggregates…. (1)
Conjoined with a state not leading out, a state not leading out arises because of the root condition – conjoined with one aggregate not leading out, three aggregates…; conjoined with two aggregates…; at the moment of rebirth…. (1)
Root: two, object: two (two in every case), result: one… non-disappearance: two (positive).
Not-root: one, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: one, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: two (negative). ^7-35-312

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-346-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U455

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-347-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-348-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-349-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-349-1-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of root condition – roots leading out are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates. (The base to be worked out.) Roots leading out are a condition, by way of root condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base to be worked out.) Roots leading out are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of root condition – roots not leading out are a condition, by way of root condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness; at the moment of rebirth…. (1) ^7-35-313

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-349-2-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness leading out; aggregates leading out are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of object condition – having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones review fruit, they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for fruit and for adverting; noble ones review the defilements that have been abandoned… that have been suppressed… that they habitually indulged in before… the eye… the physical base, one sees the aggregates not leading out as impermanent… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form, with the divine ear element one hears a sound, by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness not leading out; the base of infinite space is a condition for the base of infinite consciousness… the base of nothingness for the base of neither perception nor non-perception… the visible-form base for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness… aggregates not leading out are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of supernormal powers, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting.
A state not leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of object condition – nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for the path. (2) ^7-35-314

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-349-3-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance leading out is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight. Co-arising predominance – predominance leading out is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. (The base to be worked out.) Predominance leading out is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-315

A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, giving it weight, enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna…; noble ones review fruit, giving it weight, they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing and for fruit; the eye… the physical base, giving the aggregates not leading out weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not leading out is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. A state not leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the path. (2) ^7-35-316

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-349-4-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of proximity condition – the path is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for fruit. (1)
A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not leading out are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not leading out; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… fruit for fruit… conformity for the attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. A state not leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of proximity condition… change-of-lineage for the path; cleansing is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the path. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-317

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-349-5-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of strong-support condition. Natural strong-support – the first path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the second path… the third path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on the path, noble ones produce an attainment that has not yet arisen, enter one that has arisen, and see formations as impermanent… with insight; the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the noble ones' analytical knowledge of meaning… analytical knowledge of ready expression and for skill in what is possible and what is impossible; the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-318

A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not leading out, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna… insight… higher knowledge… produces an attainment… fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not leading out… wisdom… lust… longing… climate… food… lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith not leading out… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not leading out… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the attainment of fruition. A state not leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – the preliminary work for the first path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the first path… the preliminary work for the fourth path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. (2) ^7-35-319

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-349-6-0

A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of pre-arising condition… two (to be worked out like the Immaterial Pair)… by way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… two. ^7-35-320

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-349-7-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of kamma condition – volition leading out is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition leading out is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. From a different moment – volition leading out is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for fruit. (The base to be worked out) volition leading out is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-321

A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising (abbreviated). From a different moment – volition not leading out is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma… by way of result condition… one only… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two… by way of dissociation condition… three (to be worked out like the Immaterial Pair)… by way of presence condition… seven (to be worked out like the Immaterial Pair; the items touched are different terms only)… by way of absence condition… three… by way of disappearance condition… three… by way of non-disappearance condition… seven. ^7-35-322

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-350-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-350-1-0

Root: four, object: three, predominance: five, proximity: three, contiguity: three, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: four, result: one, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: three, disappearance: three, non-disappearance: seven.
The positive method. ^7-35-323

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-350-2-0

A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. A state leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition. A state leading out is a condition for a state leading out and a state not leading out by way of co-arising condition. (3) ^7-35-324

A state not leading out is a condition for a state not leading out by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state not leading out is a condition for a state leading out by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state leading out and a state not leading out are a condition for a state leading out: co-arising, pre-arising. A state leading out and a state not leading out are a condition for a state not leading out: co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (3) ^7-35-325

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-351-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-351-1-0

Not-root: seven, not-object: seven, not-predominance: seven, not-proximity, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: five, not-mutuality: five, not-support: five, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: six, not-post-arising: seven… not-association: five, not-dissociation: four, not-presence: four, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-35-326

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-352-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: four, not-proximity, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four… not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-327

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-353-0

With not-root as condition – object: three, predominance: five (the positive list of headings should be expanded)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Leading-Out Pair is finished. ^7-35-328

###### The Fixed Pair ^7-4-1-1-354-0

###### 1-6. The Dependence Section and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-355-0

Dependent on a state fixed, a state fixed arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate fixed, three aggregates…; dependent on two aggregates…. Dependent on a state fixed, a state not fixed arises because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates fixed, matter produced by consciousness (abbreviated. All five questions should be worked out. As in the Leading-Out Pair, so the Dependence Section, the Co-arising Section, the Condition Section, the Support Section, the Conjoined Section and the Association Section should be worked out, without difference; the items touched differ). ^7-35-329

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-356-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-357-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-358-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-358-1-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of root condition… four (the same as the Leading-Out Pair, without difference). ^7-35-330

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-358-2-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of object condition – noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, they review the defilements fixed that have been abandoned, the defilements they habitually indulged in before… they see the aggregates fixed as impermanent… with insight; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness fixed; aggregates fixed are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (1)
A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of object condition – after giving a gift, undertaking the precepts… carrying out the observance-day duties… good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna, one reviews the jhāna, enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust not fixed arises, wrong view… doubt… restlessness… distress not fixed arises; noble ones review fruit, they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for fruit and for adverting; noble ones review the defilements not fixed that have been abandoned, the defilements that have been suppressed… that they habitually indulged in before… the eye… the physical base, they see the aggregates not fixed as impermanent… with insight, they enjoy and delight in them; with that as object, lust not fixed… distress arises; with the divine eye one sees a visible form… is a condition, by way of object condition, for adverting. A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of object condition; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for the path; the material life faculty is a condition, by way of object condition, for the act of killing one's mother… the act of killing one's father… the act of killing an arahant… the act of drawing the Buddha's blood; for one who grasps at a physical base, aggregates fixed in wrongness arise; that physical base is a condition, by way of object condition, for the aggregates fixed in wrongness. (2) ^7-35-331

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-358-3-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance fixed is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight. Co-arising predominance – predominance fixed is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed and a state not fixed by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance fixed is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-332

A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – having given a gift, undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, giving it weight, enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust not fixed arises… wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones review fruit, giving it weight, they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing and for fruit; the eye… the physical base, giving the aggregates not fixed weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust not fixed arises, wrong view arises. Co-arising predominance – predominance not fixed is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the path. (2) ^7-35-333

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-358-4-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of proximity condition – the path is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for fruit; aggregates fixed are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (1)
A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates not fixed are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates not fixed; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity for cleansing… fruit for fruit… conformity for the attainment of fruition… for one emerging from cessation, the base of neither perception nor non-perception is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of proximity condition – distress not fixed for distress fixed, wrong view not fixed is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for fixed wrong view; change-of-lineage for the path… cleansing is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the path. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-334

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-358-5-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of strong-support condition. Natural strong-support – the act of killing one's mother is a condition for the act of killing one's mother… for the act of killing one's father… for the act of killing an arahant… for the act of drawing the Buddha's blood… for the act of splitting the monastic community, and for fixed wrong view, by way of strong-support condition (the cycle). The first path is a condition for the second path… the third path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – having taken the life of one's mother… having split the monastic community, in order to counteract it one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts, carries out the observance-day duties; noble ones, relying on the path, produce an attainment that has not yet arisen, enter one that has arisen… the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for skill in what is possible and what is impossible; the path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-335

A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith not fixed, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight… higher knowledge… produces an attainment, fosters conceit… adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue not fixed… wisdom… lust… longing… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment, kills a living being… raids a town; faith not fixed… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith not fixed… for longing… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain, for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust not fixed, one takes the life of one's mother… splits the monastic community; relying on hatred not fixed… lodging, one takes the life of one's mother… splits the monastic community; lust not fixed… hatred… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the act of killing one's mother… for the act of splitting the monastic community; the preliminary work for the first path is a condition for the first path… the preliminary work for the fourth path is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for the fourth path. (2) ^7-35-336

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-358-6-0

A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base (abbreviated). A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the material life faculty is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the act of killing one's mother… the act of killing one's father… the act of killing an arahant… the act of drawing the Buddha's blood. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for aggregates fixed. (2)
By way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… two. ^7-35-337

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-358-7-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of kamma condition – volition fixed is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition fixed is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. From a different moment – volition fixed is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (The base to be worked out.) Volition fixed is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment (abbreviated).
By way of result condition… one… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two… by way of dissociation condition… three (the same as the Immaterial Pair)… by way of presence condition… seven (the same as the Immaterial-Sphere Pair)… by way of absence condition… by way of disappearance condition… by way of non-disappearance condition… seven. ^7-35-338

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-359-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-359-1-0

Root: four, object: three, predominance: five, proximity: three, contiguity: three, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: four, result: one, nutriment: four, faculty, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: three, disappearance: three, non-disappearance: seven. ^7-35-339

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-359-2-0

A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. A state fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. A state fixed is a condition for a state fixed and a state not fixed by way of co-arising condition. (3) ^7-35-340

A state not fixed is a condition for a state not fixed by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state not fixed is a condition for a state fixed by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state fixed and a state not fixed are a condition for a state fixed: co-arising, pre-arising. A state fixed and a state not fixed are a condition for a state not fixed: co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-35-341

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-360-0

####### *The Counting* ^7-4-1-1-360-1-0

Not-root: seven, not-object: seven, not-predominance: seven, not-proximity, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: five, not-mutuality: five, not-support: five, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: six, not-post-arising: seven… not-association: five, not-dissociation: four, not-presence: four, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-35-342

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-361-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: four, not-proximity, not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four… not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-343

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-362-0

With not-root as condition – object: three, predominance: five (the positive list of headings should be expanded)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Fixed Pair is finished. ^7-35-344

###### The Surpassable Pair ^7-4-1-1-363-0

###### 1-7. The Dependence Section and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-364-0

Dependent on a state surpassable, a state surpassable arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate surpassable, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness…; dependent on two aggregates…; at the moment of rebirth…. (Up to the internal great elements. As in the mundane pair in the Minor Intermediate Pairs, the same, without difference.)
The Surpassable Pair is finished. ^7-35-345

###### The Conflict Pair ^7-4-1-1-365-0

###### The Dependence Section ^7-4-1-1-366-0

###### 1-4. The Positive Method of the Conditions and the Rest ^7-4-1-1-367-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-367-1-0

Dependent on a state with conflict, a state with conflict arises because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate with conflict, three aggregates…; dependent on two aggregates…. (The five questions are the same as the Immaterial-Sphere Pair, the same as the positive dependence.)
Root: five, object: two, predominance: five… non-disappearance: five.
The positive method.
Dependent on a state with conflict, a state with conflict arises not because of the root condition – dependent on the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Dependent on a state without conflict, a state without conflict arises not because of the root condition – dependent on one aggregate without roots and without conflict, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness…; dependent on two aggregates…; at the moment of rebirth without roots… (up to non-percipient beings). (1)
Not-root: two, not-object: three, not-predominance: five, not-proximity: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: five, not-repetition: five, not-kamma: two, not-result: five, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three.
The negative method. ^7-35-346

###### The Co-arising Section ^7-4-1-1-368-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Co-arising Section should be worked out.) ^7-U456

###### The Condition Section ^7-4-1-1-369-0

####### *1-4. The Fourfold Series of the Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-369-1-0

With a state with conflict as condition, a state with conflict arises because of the root condition – with one aggregate with conflict as condition, three aggregates…; with two aggregates… (the Condition Section should be worked out in the same way as for the Immaterial-Sphere Pair).
Root: nine, object: four, predominance: nine… non-disappearance: nine.
The positive method. ^7-35-347

With a state with conflict as condition, a state with conflict arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
With a state without conflict as condition, a state without conflict arises not because of the root condition – with one aggregate without roots and without conflict as condition, three aggregates and matter produced by consciousness…; with two aggregates…; at the moment of rebirth without roots… (up to non-percipient beings) with the eye base as condition, eye-consciousness… with the body base as condition, body-consciousness; with the physical base as condition, aggregates without roots. With a state without conflict as condition, a state with conflict arises not because of the root condition – with the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (2)
With a state with conflict and a state without conflict as condition, a state with conflict arises not because of the root condition – with the aggregates accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness and the physical base as condition, delusion accompanied by doubt and accompanied by restlessness. (1)
Not-root: four, not-object: three, not-predominance: nine, not-proximity: three, not-contiguity: three, not-mutuality: three, not-strong-support: three, not-pre-arising: four, not-post-arising: nine, not-repetition: nine, not-kamma: four, not-result: nine, not-nutriment: one, not-faculty: one, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-association: three, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: three, not-disappearance: three.
The negative method. ^7-35-348

###### 4-5. The Support and Conjoined Sections ^7-4-1-1-370-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Support Section should be worked out; in the Conjoined Section, two questions should be worked out in every case.)
Root: two, object: two (two in every case), result: one, non-disappearance: two (positive).
Not-root: two, not-predominance: two, not-pre-arising: two, not-post-arising: two, not-repetition: two, not-kamma: two, not-result: two, not-jhāna: one, not-path: one, not-dissociation: two (negative). ^7-U457

###### The Association Section ^7-4-1-1-371-0

(In the same way, the other two, the counting as well, and the Association Section should be worked out.) ^7-U458

###### The Analysis Section ^7-4-1-1-372-0

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-373-0

###### The Exposition ^7-4-1-1-374-0

####### *The Root Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-1-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of root condition (the same as the Immaterial Pair, four). ^7-35-349

####### *The Object Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-2-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of object condition – one enjoys and delights in lust; with that as object, lust arises, wrong view… doubt… restlessness… distress arises; one enjoys and delights in wrong view; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; with doubt as object… with restlessness as object… with distress as object, distress arises, wrong view… doubt… restlessness arises. A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of object condition – noble ones review the defilements that have been abandoned… that have been suppressed… that they habitually indulged in before… they see the aggregates with conflict as impermanent… with insight; by knowledge of others' minds one knows the consciousness of one who possesses consciousness with conflict. Aggregates with conflict are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of others' minds, for knowledge of recollecting past lives, for knowledge of how beings fare according to their kamma, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. (2) ^7-35-350

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of object condition; having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path; fruit… they review nibbāna; nibbāna is a condition, by way of object condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path, for fruit and for adverting. The eye… the physical base, one sees the aggregates without conflict as impermanent… with insight, with the divine eye one sees a visible form… they are a condition, by way of object condition, for knowledge of the future and for adverting. A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of object condition – having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… the physical base, one enjoys and delights in the aggregates without conflict; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises. (2) ^7-35-351

####### *The Predominance Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-3-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – giving lust weight, one enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises… wrong view arises; giving wrong view weight, one enjoys and delights in it. Co-arising predominance – predominance with conflict is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates. A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with conflict is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for matter produced by consciousness. A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict and a state without conflict by way of predominance condition. Co-arising predominance – predominance with conflict is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3) ^7-35-352

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of predominance condition – object-predominance, co-arising predominance. Object-predominance – having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, one reviews it, giving it weight; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… noble ones, having emerged from the path, review the path, giving it weight; fruit… they review nibbāna, giving it weight; nibbāna is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for change-of-lineage, for cleansing, for the path and for fruit. Co-arising predominance – predominance without conflict is a condition, by way of predominance condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of predominance condition. Object-predominance – having given a gift… having undertaken the precepts… having carried out the observance-day duties, giving it weight, one enjoys and delights in it; giving that weight, lust arises… wrong view arises; good deeds done in the past… having emerged from jhāna… the eye… the physical base, giving the aggregates without conflict weight, one enjoys and delights in them; giving that weight, lust arises… wrong view arises. (2) ^7-35-353

####### *The Proximity Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-374-4-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates with conflict are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates with conflict. A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of proximity condition – aggregates with conflict are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for emergence. (2) ^7-35-354

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of proximity condition – preceding aggregates without conflict are a condition, by way of proximity condition, for following aggregates without conflict; conformity for change-of-lineage… conformity is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the attainment of fruition. A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of proximity condition – adverting is a condition, by way of proximity condition, for the aggregates with conflict. (2)
By way of contiguity condition… by way of co-arising condition… five… by way of mutuality condition… two… by way of support condition… seven. ^7-35-355

####### *The Strong-Support Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-5-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one kills a living being… splits the monastic community; relying on hatred… longing, one kills a living being… splits the monastic community; lust… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for longing. A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of strong-support condition – proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on lust, one gives a gift, undertakes the precepts… carries out the observance-day duties, produces jhāna, produces insight… the path… higher knowledge… produces an attainment; relying on hatred… longing, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; lust… longing is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for wisdom… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path… for the attainment of fruition. (2) ^7-35-356

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; relying on virtue… wisdom… bodily pleasure… bodily pain… climate… food… lodging, one gives a gift… produces an attainment; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for faith… for wisdom… for bodily pleasure… for bodily pain… for the path… for the attainment of fruition. (1)
A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of strong-support condition – object strong-support, proximity strong-support, natural strong-support…. Natural strong-support – relying on faith, one fosters conceit, adopts a wrong view; relying on virtue… lodging, one kills a living being… splits the monastic community; faith… lodging is a condition, by way of strong-support condition, for lust… for longing. (2) ^7-35-357

####### *The Pre-arising Condition and the Rest* ^7-4-1-1-374-6-0

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… one sees the physical base as impermanent… with insight… with the divine eye one sees a visible form, with the divine ear element one hears a sound; the visible-form base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for eye-consciousness… the tangible base for body-consciousness…. Pre-arising physical base – the eye base is a condition for eye-consciousness… the body base for body-consciousness… the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates without conflict. A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of pre-arising condition – pre-arising object, pre-arising physical base. Pre-arising object – the eye… the physical base, one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises. Pre-arising physical base – the physical base is a condition, by way of pre-arising condition, for the aggregates with conflict. (2)
By way of post-arising condition… two… by way of repetition condition… two. ^7-35-358

####### *The Kamma Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-7-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of kamma condition – volition with conflict is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates. (The base to be worked out.) Co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – volition with conflict is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for matter produced by consciousness. From a different moment – volition with conflict is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the resultant aggregates and for matter produced by kamma. (The base to be worked out.) Volition with conflict is a condition, by way of kamma condition, for the associated aggregates and for matter produced by consciousness. (3)
A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of kamma condition – co-arising, from a different moment. Co-arising – (abbreviated). (1)
By way of result condition… one… by way of nutriment condition… four… by way of faculty condition… four… by way of jhāna condition… four… by way of path condition… four… by way of association condition… two… by way of dissociation condition… three (the same as the Immaterial Pair). ^7-35-359

####### *The Presence Condition* ^7-4-1-1-374-8-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of presence condition (abbreviated). A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising (abbreviated). A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict and a state without conflict by way of presence condition (abbreviated). (3) ^7-35-360

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty (abbreviated). A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of presence condition. Pre-arising – the eye… the physical base, one enjoys and delights in it; with that as object, lust arises… distress arises; the physical base is a condition, by way of presence condition, for the aggregates with conflict. (2)
A state with conflict and a state without conflict are a condition for a state with conflict by way of presence condition – co-arising, pre-arising (abbreviated). A state with conflict and a state without conflict are a condition for a state without conflict by way of presence condition – co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. Co-arising – aggregates with conflict and the great elements are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by consciousness. Post-arising – aggregates with conflict and physical food are a condition, by way of presence condition, for this body. Post-arising – aggregates with conflict and the material life faculty are a condition, by way of presence condition, for matter produced by kamma. (2) ^7-35-361

###### Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-375-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-376-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-376-1-0

Root: four, object: four, predominance: five, proximity: four, contiguity: four, co-arising: five, mutuality: two, support: seven, strong-support: four, pre-arising: two, post-arising: two, repetition: two, kamma: four, result: one, nutriment: four, faculty: four, jhāna: four, path: four, association: two, dissociation: three, presence: seven, absence: four, disappearance: four, non-disappearance: seven. ^7-35-362

####### *Extraction for the Negative Method* ^7-4-1-1-376-2-0

A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition. A state with conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition. A state with conflict is a condition for a state with conflict and a state without conflict by way of co-arising condition. (3) ^7-35-363

A state without conflict is a condition for a state without conflict by way of object condition… by way of co-arising condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition… by way of post-arising condition… by way of kamma condition… by way of nutriment condition… by way of faculty condition. A state without conflict is a condition for a state with conflict by way of object condition… by way of strong-support condition… by way of pre-arising condition. (2)
A state with conflict and a state without conflict are a condition for a state with conflict: co-arising, pre-arising. A state with conflict and a state without conflict are a condition for a state without conflict: co-arising, post-arising, nutriment, faculty. (2) ^7-35-364

###### Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-377-0

###### The Counting ^7-4-1-1-378-0

####### *Single Conditions* ^7-4-1-1-378-1-0

Not-root: seven, not-contiguity: seven, not-co-arising: five, not-mutuality: five, not-support: five, not-strong-support: seven, not-pre-arising: six, not-post-arising: seven… not-association: five, not-dissociation: four, not-presence: four, not-absence: seven, not-disappearance: seven, not-non-disappearance: four. ^7-35-365

###### Positive-Negative Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-379-0

With root as condition – not-object: four, not-predominance: four… not-contiguity: four, not-mutuality: two, not-strong-support: four… not-association: two, not-dissociation: two, not-absence: four, not-disappearance: four. ^7-35-366

###### Negative-Positive Method of the Conditions ^7-4-1-1-380-0

With not-root as condition – object: four, predominance: five, proximity: four (the positive list of headings should be taken)… non-disappearance: seven.
The Conflict Pair is finished.
The Final Pairs are finished.
The Conditional Relations of the Pairs in the Positive Method by States is finished. ^7-35-367
